To elaborate, the final three terms result from applying integration by parts to stochastic integrals,
with the last term specifically using the formula in (
3.3b
).

We proceed to the weak formulations of both sides of (
4.23
).
With
{g}={g}(y,x,s,T)
to lighten notation, integration against
{g}{\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s
over
0\leq s\leq T
and
y,x\in{\mathbb{R}}^{2}
for both sides of (
4.23
) yields

\displaystyle\int_{s=0}^{T}\int_{x}\int_{y}{g}[X_{\varepsilon}({\varepsilon}y+x,s)-X_{\varepsilon}(x,s)]X_{\varepsilon}(x,s){\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s

\displaystyle\quad=I^{2,1}_{{\varepsilon}}({g},T)+\tilde{I}^{2,2}_{{\varepsilon}}({g},T)+\tilde{I}^{2,3}_{{\varepsilon}}({g},T)+\tilde{I}^{2,4}_{{\varepsilon}}({g},T)+\tilde{I}^{2,5}_{{\varepsilon}}({g},T)+\tilde{I}^{2,6}_{{\varepsilon}}({g},T),

where
I^{2,1}_{{\varepsilon}}({g},T)
has been defined below (
4.21
), and the remaining terms are defined as folllows:

\displaystyle\tilde{I}^{2,2}_{{\varepsilon}}({g},T)

\displaystyle\,{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\rm def}}{{=}}}\,\int_{s=0}^{T}\int_{x}\int_{y}\int_{r^{\prime}=0}^{s}\int_{y^{\prime}}{g}[P_{s-r^{\prime}}({\varepsilon}y+x,y^{\prime})-P_{s-r^{\prime}}(x,y^{\prime})]P_{s}X_{0}(x){M}_{\varepsilon}({\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime},{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime}){\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s,

\displaystyle\tilde{I}^{2,3}_{{\varepsilon}}({g},T)

\displaystyle\,{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\rm def}}{{=}}}\,\int_{s=0}^{T}\int_{x}\int_{y}\int_{r^{\prime}=0}^{s}\int_{y^{\prime}}{g}[P_{s}X_{0}({\varepsilon}y+x)-P_{s}X_{0}(x)]P_{s-r^{\prime}}(x,y^{\prime}){M}_{\varepsilon}({\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime},{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime}){\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s,

\displaystyle\tilde{I}^{2,4}_{{\varepsilon}}({g},T)

\displaystyle\,{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\rm def}}{{=}}}\,\int_{s=0}^{T}\int_{x}\int_{y}\int_{r^{\prime}=0}^{s}\int_{y^{\prime}}{g}[P_{s-r^{\prime}}({\varepsilon}y+x,y^{\prime})-P_{s-r^{\prime}}(x,y^{\prime})]

\displaystyle\quad\times\biggl(\int_{r^{\prime\prime}=0}^{r^{\prime}}\int_{y^{\prime\prime}}P_{s-r^{\prime\prime}}(x,y^{\prime\prime}){M}_{\varepsilon}({\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime},{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime\prime})\biggr){M}_{\varepsilon}({\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime},{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime}){\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s,

\displaystyle\tilde{I}^{2,5}_{{\varepsilon}}({g},T)

\displaystyle\,{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\rm def}}{{=}}}\,\int_{s=0}^{T}\int_{x}\int_{y}\int_{r^{\prime\prime}=0}^{s}\int_{y^{\prime\prime}}{g}\biggl(\int_{r^{\prime}=0}^{r^{\prime\prime}}\int_{y^{\prime}}[P_{s-r^{\prime}}({\varepsilon}y+x,y^{\prime})-P_{s-r^{\prime}}(x,y^{\prime})]{M}_{\varepsilon}({\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime},{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime})\biggr)

\displaystyle\quad\times P_{s-r^{\prime\prime}}(x,y^{\prime\prime}){M}_{\varepsilon}({\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime},{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime\prime}){\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s,

\displaystyle\tilde{I}^{2,6}_{{\varepsilon}}({g},T)

\displaystyle\,{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\rm def}}{{=}}}\,\int_{s=0}^{T}\int_{x}\int_{y}\int_{r^{\prime}=0}^{s}\int_{y^{\prime}}\int_{y^{\prime\prime}}{g}[P_{s-r^{\prime}}({\varepsilon}y+x,{\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}+y^{\prime})-P_{s-r^{\prime}}(x,{\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}+y^{\prime})]P_{s-r^{\prime}}(x,y^{\prime})

\displaystyle\quad\times\Lambda_{\varepsilon}X_{\varepsilon}({\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}+y^{\prime},r^{\prime})X_{\varepsilon}(y^{\prime},r^{\prime})\phi(y^{\prime\prime}){\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime}{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime}{\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s.

By the Fubini theorem,

\tilde{I}^{2,6}_{\varepsilon}({g},T)=I^{2,4}_{\varepsilon}({g},T)
.
To complete the proof of (
4.21
), we show below that

\displaystyle\tilde{I}^{2,2}_{{\varepsilon}}({g},T)+\tilde{I}^{2,4}_{{\varepsilon}}({g},T)

\displaystyle=I^{2,2}_{{\varepsilon}}({g},T),

(4.24)

\displaystyle\tilde{I}^{2,3}_{{\varepsilon}}({g},T)+\tilde{I}^{2,5}_{{\varepsilon}}({g},T)

\displaystyle=I^{2,3}_{{\varepsilon}}({g},T).

(4.25)

To prove (
4.24
), we first consider the following equality, which is obtained by
applying the Chapman–Kolmorgorov equation to
P_{s-r^{\prime\prime}}(x,y^{\prime\prime})
:

\displaystyle\tilde{I}^{2,4}_{{\varepsilon}}({g},T)

\displaystyle=\int_{s=0}^{T}\int_{x}\int_{y}\int_{r^{\prime}=0}^{s}\int_{y^{\prime}}{g}[P_{s-r^{\prime}}({\varepsilon}y+x,y^{\prime})-P_{s-r^{\prime}}(x,y^{\prime})]

\displaystyle\quad\biggl(\int_{r^{\prime\prime}=0}^{r^{\prime}}\int_{y^{\prime\prime}}\int_{z^{\prime\prime}}P_{s-r^{\prime}}(x,z^{\prime\prime})P_{r^{\prime}-r^{\prime\prime}}(z^{\prime\prime},y^{\prime\prime}){\mathrm{d}}z^{\prime\prime}{M}_{\varepsilon}({\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime},{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime\prime})\biggr){M}_{\varepsilon}({\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime},{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime}){\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s

\displaystyle=\int_{s=0}^{T}\int_{x}\int_{y}\int_{r^{\prime}=0}^{s}\int_{y^{\prime}}{g}[P_{s-r^{\prime}}({\varepsilon}y+x,y^{\prime})-P_{s-r^{\prime}}(x,y^{\prime})]

\displaystyle\quad\biggl(\int_{z^{\prime\prime}}P_{s-r^{\prime}}(x,z^{\prime\prime})\int_{r^{\prime\prime}=0}^{r^{\prime}}\int_{y^{\prime\prime}}P_{r^{\prime}-r^{\prime\prime}}(z^{\prime\prime},y^{\prime\prime}){M}_{\varepsilon}({\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime},{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime\prime}){\mathrm{d}}z^{\prime\prime}\biggr){M}_{\varepsilon}({\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime},{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime}){\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s.

Here, the final equality employs the version of the stochastic Fubini theorem found in
[
65
, Theorem 2.6, pp.296–297]
. The required
L^{2}
condition for that theorem in
[
65
]
is satisfied because, for any fixed
0<r^{\prime}<s
and
x\in{\mathbb{R}}^{2}
,
P_{s-r^{\prime}}(x,z^{\prime\prime})\,dz^{\prime\prime}
over
z^{\prime\prime}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{2}
defines a finite measure, and

\displaystyle\int_{z^{\prime\prime}}\int_{r^{\prime\prime}=0}^{r^{\prime}}\int_{y^{\prime\prime}}\int_{y^{\prime\prime\prime}}P_{s-r^{\prime}}(x,z^{\prime\prime})P_{r^{\prime}-r^{\prime\prime}}(z^{\prime\prime},y^{\prime\prime\prime})P_{r^{\prime}-r^{\prime\prime}}(z^{\prime\prime},y^{\prime\prime})\langle M_{\varepsilon},M_{\varepsilon}{\rangle}({\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime\prime},{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime},{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime\prime}){\mathrm{d}}z^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle\quad=\int_{z^{\prime\prime}}\int_{r^{\prime\prime}=0}^{r^{\prime}}\int_{y^{\prime\prime}}\int_{\widetilde{y}^{\prime\prime}}P_{s-r^{\prime}}(x,z^{\prime\prime})P_{r^{\prime}-r^{\prime\prime}}(z^{\prime\prime},{\varepsilon}\widetilde{y}^{\prime\prime}+y^{\prime\prime})P_{r^{\prime}-r^{\prime\prime}}(z^{\prime\prime},y^{\prime\prime})\phi(\widetilde{y}^{\prime\prime}){\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{y}^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}z^{\prime\prime}<\infty,

where the last inequality follows upon applying Lemma
3.3
.

To proceed, we use the mild form (
2.18
) for a further representation of
\tilde{I}^{2,4}_{{\varepsilon}}({g},T)
:

\displaystyle\tilde{I}^{2,4}_{{\varepsilon}}({g},T)

\displaystyle=\int_{s=0}^{T}\int_{x}\int_{y}\int_{r^{\prime}=0}^{s}\int_{y^{\prime}}{g}[P_{s-r^{\prime}}({\varepsilon}y+x,y^{\prime})-P_{s-r^{\prime}}(x,y^{\prime})]

\displaystyle\quad\left(\int_{z^{\prime\prime}}P_{s-r^{\prime}}(x,z^{\prime\prime})[X_{\varepsilon}(z^{\prime\prime},r^{\prime})-P_{r^{\prime}}X_{0}(z^{\prime\prime})]{\mathrm{d}}z^{\prime\prime}\right){M}_{\varepsilon}({\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime},{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime}){\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s

\displaystyle=\int_{s=0}^{T}\int_{x}\int_{y}\int_{r^{\prime}=0}^{s}\int_{y^{\prime}}{g}[P_{s-r^{\prime}}({\varepsilon}y+x,y^{\prime})-P_{s-r^{\prime}}(x,y^{\prime})]

\displaystyle\quad\times\int_{z^{\prime\prime}}P_{s-r^{\prime}}(x,z^{\prime\prime})X_{\varepsilon}(z^{\prime\prime},r^{\prime}){\mathrm{d}}z^{\prime\prime}{M}_{\varepsilon}({\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime},{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime}){\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s

\displaystyle\quad-\int_{s=0}^{T}\int_{x}\int_{y}\int_{r^{\prime}=0}^{s}\int_{y^{\prime}}{g}[P_{s-r^{\prime}}({\varepsilon}y+x,y^{\prime})-P_{s-r^{\prime}}(x,y^{\prime})]

\displaystyle\quad\times P_{s}X_{0}(x){M}_{\varepsilon}({\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime},{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime}){\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s,

where the last equality holds since
\int_{z^{\prime\prime}}P_{s-r^{\prime}}(x,z^{\prime\prime})P_{r^{\prime}}X_{0}(z^{\prime\prime}){\mathrm{d}}z^{\prime\prime}=P_{s}X_{0}(x)
for
0<r^{\prime}<s
. Since the last term equals
\tilde{I}^{2,2}_{{\varepsilon}}({g},T)
, we get

\displaystyle\tilde{I}^{2,2}_{{\varepsilon}}({g},T)+\tilde{I}^{2,4}_{{\varepsilon}}({g},T)

\displaystyle=\int_{s=0}^{T}\int_{x}\int_{y}\int_{r^{\prime}=0}^{s}\int_{y^{\prime}}{g}[P_{s-r^{\prime}}({\varepsilon}y+x,y^{\prime})-P_{s-r^{\prime}}(x,y^{\prime})]

\displaystyle\quad\times\left(\int_{z^{\prime\prime}}P_{s-r^{\prime}}(x,z^{\prime\prime})X_{\varepsilon}(z^{\prime\prime},r^{\prime}){\mathrm{d}}z^{\prime\prime}\right){M}_{\varepsilon}({\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime},{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime}){\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s

\displaystyle=\int_{r^{\prime}=0}^{T}\int_{y^{\prime}}\int_{s=r^{\prime}}^{T}\int_{x}\int_{y}{g}[P_{s-r^{\prime}}({\varepsilon}y+x,y^{\prime})-P_{s-r^{\prime}}(x,y^{\prime})]

\displaystyle\quad\times\int_{z^{\prime\prime}}P_{s-r^{\prime}}(x,z^{\prime\prime})X_{\varepsilon}(z^{\prime\prime},r^{\prime}){\mathrm{d}}z^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s{M}_{\varepsilon}({\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime},{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime}).

Here, the final equality is justified by the stochastic Fubini theorem, as in the preceding paragraph, now also invoking Proposition
3.1
(2
∘
).
In particular, we interchange the order of integration so that

M_{\varepsilon}({\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime},{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime})g(y,x,s,T)^{\pm}{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}s=g(y,x,s,T)^{\pm}{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}sM_{\varepsilon}({\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime},{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime}),

where
g^{\pm}
are the positive and negative parts of
g
.
Equation (
4.24
) then follows by applying the Fubini theorem to the integrand of
M_{\varepsilon}({\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime},{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime})
, using
{\mathrm{d}}z^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s={\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s{\mathrm{d}}z^{\prime\prime}
.

The proof of (
4.25
) follows a similar argument. To rewrite
\tilde{I}^{2,5}_{{\varepsilon}}({g},T)
, we again use the Chapman-Kolmogorov equation to decompose
P_{s-r^{\prime}}({\varepsilon}y+x,y^{\prime})
and
P_{s-r^{\prime}}(x,y^{\prime})
, followed by applications of the stochastic Fubini theorem. This produces the first equality below:

\displaystyle\tilde{I}^{2,5}_{{\varepsilon}}({g},T)

\displaystyle=\int_{s=0}^{T}\int_{x}\int_{y}\int_{r^{\prime\prime}=0}^{s}\int_{y^{\prime\prime}}{g}\biggl(\int_{z^{\prime}}P_{s-r^{\prime\prime}}({\varepsilon}y+x,z^{\prime})\int_{r^{\prime}=0}^{r^{\prime\prime}}\int_{y^{\prime}}P_{r^{\prime\prime}-r^{\prime}}(z^{\prime},y^{\prime}){M}_{\varepsilon}({\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime},{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime}){\mathrm{d}}z^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad-\int_{z^{\prime}}P_{s-r^{\prime\prime}}(x,z^{\prime})\int_{r^{\prime}=0}^{r^{\prime\prime}}\int_{y^{\prime}}P_{r^{\prime\prime}-r^{\prime}}(z^{\prime},y^{\prime}){M}_{\varepsilon}({\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime},{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime}){\mathrm{d}}z^{\prime}\biggr)

\displaystyle\quad\times P_{s-r^{\prime\prime}}(x,y^{\prime\prime}){M}_{\varepsilon}({\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime},{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime\prime}){\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s

\displaystyle=\int_{s=0}^{T}\int_{x}\int_{y}\int_{r^{\prime\prime}=0}^{s}\int_{y^{\prime\prime}}{g}\biggl(\int_{z^{\prime}}P_{s-r^{\prime\prime}}({\varepsilon}y+x,z^{\prime})[X_{\varepsilon}(z^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime})-P_{r^{\prime\prime}}X_{0}(z^{\prime})]{\mathrm{d}}z^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad-\int_{z^{\prime}}P_{s-r^{\prime\prime}}(x,z^{\prime})[X_{\varepsilon}(z^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime})-P_{r^{\prime\prime}}X_{0}(z^{\prime})]{\mathrm{d}}z^{\prime}\biggr)P_{s-r^{\prime\prime}}(x,y^{\prime\prime}){M}_{\varepsilon}({\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime},{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime\prime}){\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s,

where the last equality follows from the mild form (
2.18
) of
X_{\varepsilon}
. To simplify the right-hand side, we apply the Chapman–Kolmogorov equation to get
\int_{z^{\prime}}P_{s-r^{\prime\prime}}(\widetilde{y},z^{\prime})P_{r^{\prime\prime}}X_{0}(z^{\prime}){\mathrm{d}}z^{\prime}=P_{s}X_{0}(\widetilde{y})
for
\widetilde{y}\in\{{\varepsilon}y+x,x\}
and
0<r^{\prime\prime}<s
.
Hence, after rearrangement, the last equality gives

\displaystyle\tilde{I}^{2,5}_{{\varepsilon}}({g},T)

\displaystyle=\int_{s=0}^{T}\int_{x}\int_{y}\int_{r^{\prime\prime}=0}^{s}\int_{y^{\prime\prime}}{g}\biggl(\int_{z^{\prime}}[P_{s-r^{\prime\prime}}({\varepsilon}y+x,z^{\prime})-P_{s-r^{\prime\prime}}(x,z^{\prime})]X_{\varepsilon}(z^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime}){\mathrm{d}}z^{\prime}\biggr)

\displaystyle\quad\times P_{s-r^{\prime\prime}}(x,y^{\prime\prime}){M}_{\varepsilon}({\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime},{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime\prime}){\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s

\displaystyle\quad-\int_{s=0}^{T}\int_{x}\int_{y}\int_{r^{\prime\prime}=0}^{s}\int_{y^{\prime\prime}}{g}[P_{s}X_{0}({\varepsilon}y+x)-P_{s}X_{0}(x)]P_{s-r^{\prime\prime}}(x,y^{\prime\prime}){M}_{\varepsilon}({\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime},{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime\prime}){\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s.

Since the last integral equals
\tilde{I}^{2,3}_{{\varepsilon}}({g},T)
, we get

\displaystyle\tilde{I}^{2,3}_{{\varepsilon}}({g},T)+\tilde{I}^{2,5}_{{\varepsilon}}({g},T)

\displaystyle\quad=\int_{s=0}^{T}\int_{x}\int_{y}\int_{r^{\prime\prime}=0}^{s}\int_{y^{\prime\prime}}{g}\left(\int_{z^{\prime}}[P_{s-r^{\prime\prime}}({\varepsilon}y+x,z^{\prime})-P_{s-r^{\prime\prime}}(x,z^{\prime})]X_{\varepsilon}(z^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime}){\mathrm{d}}z^{\prime}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times P_{s-r^{\prime\prime}}(x,y^{\prime\prime}){M}_{\varepsilon}({\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime},{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime\prime}){\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s

\displaystyle\quad=\int_{r^{\prime\prime}=0}^{T}\int_{y^{\prime\prime}}\int_{s=r^{\prime\prime}}^{T}\int_{x}\int_{y}{g}\left(\int_{z^{\prime}}[P_{s-r^{\prime\prime}}({\varepsilon}y+x,z^{\prime})-P_{s-r^{\prime\prime}}(x,z^{\prime})]X_{\varepsilon}(z^{\prime},r^{\prime\prime}){\mathrm{d}}z^{\prime}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times P_{s-r^{\prime\prime}}(x,y^{\prime\prime}){\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s{M}_{\varepsilon}({\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime},{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime\prime}),

where the last equality applies the stochastic Fubini theorem. By applying the Fubini theorem to the last equality, we rearrange the order of integration as
{\mathrm{d}}z^{\prime}{\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s={\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s{\mathrm{d}}z^{\prime}
. This proves (
4.25
).

Finally, to justify (
4.22
), observe that the mapping
(y,x,s)\mapsto\mathfrak{L}_{\varepsilon}^{1}f(y,x,s,T)
, as defined in (
3.17
), belongs to
L^{1}({\mathbb{R}}^{2}\times{\mathbb{R}}^{2}\times[0,T])
. This integrability is ensured by the assumption
f(x,s)\in{\mathscr{C}}_{pd}^{0}({\mathbb{R}}^{2}\times[0,T])
and the inequality below, which follows from Lemma
3.3
:

\int_{s=0}^{T}\int_{x}\int_{y}\left(\int_{t=s}^{T}\int_{\widetilde{x}}\frac{1}{1+\lvert\widetilde{x}\rvert^{10}}P_{t-s}({\varepsilon}y+x,\widetilde{x})P_{t-s}(\widetilde{x},x){\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{x}{\mathrm{d}}t\phi(y)\right){\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s<\infty.

The proof is complete.

\blacksquare

The following lemma further develops the functionals
g\mapsto I^{2,k}_{\varepsilon}(g,T)
introduced in Lemma
4.4
. It also involves the remainder terms
R_{\ref{key}}(y,x,s,T)
from Lemma
4.2
, which depend on
f
via (
4.2
).

Lemma 4.5.

(1
∘
)
Let
0<T<\infty
. Suppose that
G=\{{g}_{\varepsilon}(y,x,s,T)\}_{{\varepsilon}\in(0,{{\varepsilon}_{\ref{def:ovl}}(\lambda)}]}
is a family of functions such that

x\mapsto{g}_{\varepsilon}(y,x,s,T)\in{\mathscr{C}}^{1}({\mathbb{R}}^{2})
and,
for some constant
M\in(0,\infty)
, the following bound holds for all
{\varepsilon}\in(0,{{\varepsilon}_{\ref{def:ovl}}(\lambda)}]
:

\displaystyle\max\{\lvert{g}_{\varepsilon}(y,x,s,T)\rvert,\lvert\nabla_{x}{g}_{\varepsilon}(y,x,s,T)\rvert\}\leq\frac{C({G},m)\mathds{1}_{\{\lvert y\rvert\leq M\}}}{1+\lvert x\rvert^{m}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\left[\lvert\log(T-s)\rvert+1+\lvert\log\lvert y\rvert\rvert+{\varepsilon}_{\ref{limT:glue}}\left(\frac{4(T-s){\varepsilon}^{-2}}{\lvert y\rvert^{2}}\right)\right],\;y,x\in{\mathbb{R}}^{2},\;0<s<T,\;m\in\mathbb{N}.

Then Lemma
4.4
applies to the functions
g_{\varepsilon}
, and we have the following estimates:

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\lim_{{\varepsilon}\to 0}\lvert I^{2,1}_{{\varepsilon}}({g}_{\varepsilon},T)\rvert=0,\quad\quad\;\;\lim_{{\varepsilon}\to 0}{\mathbb{E}}[\lvert I^{2,2}_{{\varepsilon}}({g}_{\varepsilon},T)\rvert^{2}]=0,\\
&\lim_{{\varepsilon}\to 0}{\mathbb{E}}[\lvert I^{2,3}_{{\varepsilon}}({g}_{\varepsilon},T)\rvert^{2}]=0,\quad\limsup_{{\varepsilon}\to 0}{\mathbb{E}}[\lvert I^{2,4}_{{\varepsilon}}({g}_{\varepsilon},T)\rvert]<\infty.\end{split}

(4.26)

(2
∘
)
For all
0<T<\infty
and
f\in{\mathscr{C}}^{1}_{pd}({\mathbb{R}}^{2}\times[0,T])
, the following estimate holds:

\displaystyle\lim_{{\varepsilon}\to 0}{\mathbb{E}}\biggl[\int_{r^{\prime}=0}^{T}\int_{y^{\prime}}\int_{y^{\prime\prime}}\int_{y}\biggl(\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{-1}\lvert R_{\ref{key}}(y^{\prime\prime}-y,y^{\prime},r^{\prime},T)\rvert+\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{-1}\lvert R_{\ref{key}}(y^{\prime\prime},y^{\prime},r^{\prime},T)\rvert\biggr)\phi(y){\mathrm{d}}y

\displaystyle\quad\times\Lambda_{{\varepsilon}}X_{\varepsilon}({\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}+y^{\prime},r^{\prime})X_{\varepsilon}(y^{\prime},r^{\prime})\phi(y^{\prime\prime}){\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime}{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime}\biggr]=0.

(4.27)

Proof.
(1
∘
) First, observe that Lemma
4.4
applies to each
g_{\varepsilon}
, since the assumed bound on
g_{\varepsilon}
together with (
4.7
) ensures
(y,x,s)\mapsto g_{\varepsilon}(y,x,s,T)\in L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{2}\times\mathbb{R}^{2}\times[0,T])
for all
{\varepsilon}\in(0,{{\varepsilon}_{\ref{def:ovl}}(\lambda)}]
. In the remainder of the proof of (1
∘
), for
j=1,2,3,4
, we establish the necessary property of
I^{2,j}_{{\varepsilon}}(g_{\varepsilon},T)
, as stated in (
4.26
), in Step
j
below. Henceforth, we abbreviate
{g}_{\varepsilon}(y,x,s,T)
as
g_{\varepsilon}
whenever the meaning is clear from context.

Step 1.
We utilize the heat kernel estimate for suitably perturbed initial conditions; see (
7.3
) with
\delta_{\varepsilon}={\varepsilon}
. Consequently,

\displaystyle\lvert I^{2,1}_{{\varepsilon}}({g}_{\varepsilon},T)\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\int_{s=0}^{{\varepsilon}\wedge T}\int_{x}\int_{y}\lvert{g}_{\varepsilon}\rvert[P_{s}X_{0}({\varepsilon}y+x)+P_{s}X_{0}(x)]P_{s}X_{0}(x){\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s

\displaystyle\quad+C(M){\varepsilon}^{1/2}\int_{s=0}^{T}\int_{x}\int_{y}\lvert{g}_{\varepsilon}\rvert P_{2s}X_{0}(x){\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s.

Observe that, by invoking the boundedness of
X_{0}(\cdot)
, the assumed bound on
\lvert{g}_{\varepsilon}\rvert
, and (
4.7
), the right-hand side approaches zero as
{\varepsilon}\to 0
. In particular, this convergence holds as we apply the following limits:

\displaystyle\lim_{{\varepsilon}\to 0}\int_{s=0}^{{\varepsilon}\wedge T}\lvert\log[4(T-s){\varepsilon}^{-2}]\rvert\mathds{1}_{\{4(T-s){\varepsilon}^{-2}<1\}}{\mathrm{d}}s=0,

\displaystyle\lim_{{\varepsilon}\to 0}{\varepsilon}^{1/2}\int_{s=0}^{T}\lvert\log[4(T-s){\varepsilon}^{-2}]\rvert\mathds{1}_{\{4(T-s){\varepsilon}^{-2}<1\}}{\mathrm{d}}s=0.

We have proved the property of
I^{2,1}_{{\varepsilon}}({g}_{\varepsilon},T)
required in (
4.26
).

Step 2.
To show that
{\mathbb{E}}[\lvert I^{2,2}_{{\varepsilon}}({g}_{\varepsilon},T)\rvert^{2}]
vanishes, as required in (
4.26
), we again apply the heat kernel estimate in (
7.3
) with
\delta_{\varepsilon}={\varepsilon}
. To carry this out, we first apply Itô’s isometry and (
3.3b
) and write

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\lvert I^{2,2}_{{\varepsilon}}({g}_{\varepsilon},T)\rvert^{2}]=I^{\mathds{1},\mathds{1}}_{{\varepsilon},\ref{I22:error}}=\sum\left\{I^{\kappa,\widetilde{\kappa}}_{{\varepsilon},\ref{I22:error}};\kappa,\widetilde{\kappa}\in\{\mathds{1}_{[0,{\varepsilon})},\mathds{1}_{[{\varepsilon},\infty)}\}\right\},

(4.28)

where
I^{\kappa,\widetilde{\kappa}}_{{\varepsilon},\ref{I22:error}}
is bilinear in
(\kappa,\widetilde{\kappa})
, defined by

\displaystyle I^{\kappa,\widetilde{\kappa}}_{{\varepsilon},\ref{I22:error}}

\displaystyle\,{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\rm def}}{{=}}}\,{\mathbb{E}}\biggl[\int_{r^{\prime}=0}^{T}\int_{y^{\prime}}\int_{y^{\prime\prime}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\biggl(\int_{z^{\prime\prime}}\int_{s=r^{\prime}}^{T}\kappa(s-r^{\prime})\int_{x}\int_{y}{g}_{\varepsilon}(y,x,s,T)[P_{s-r^{\prime}}({\varepsilon}y+x,{\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}+y^{\prime})-P_{s-r^{\prime}}(x,{\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}+y^{\prime})]

\displaystyle\quad\times P_{s-r^{\prime}}(x,z^{\prime\prime}){\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}sX_{\varepsilon}(z^{\prime\prime},r^{\prime}){\mathrm{d}}z^{\prime\prime}\biggr)

\displaystyle\quad\times\biggl(\int_{\widetilde{z}^{\prime\prime}}\int_{\widetilde{s}=r^{\prime}}^{T}\widetilde{\kappa}(\widetilde{s}-r^{\prime})\int_{\widetilde{x}}\int_{\widetilde{y}}{g}_{\varepsilon}(\widetilde{y},\widetilde{x},\widetilde{s},T)[P_{\widetilde{s}-r^{\prime}}({\varepsilon}\widetilde{y}+\widetilde{x},y^{\prime})-P_{\widetilde{s}-r^{\prime}}(\widetilde{x},y^{\prime})]

\displaystyle\quad\times P_{\widetilde{s}-r^{\prime}}(\widetilde{x},\widetilde{z}^{\prime\prime}){\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{y}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{x}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}X_{\varepsilon}(\widetilde{z}^{\prime\prime},r^{\prime}){\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{z}^{\prime\prime}\biggr)

\displaystyle\quad\times\Lambda_{\varepsilon}X_{\varepsilon}({\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}+y^{\prime},r^{\prime})X_{\varepsilon}(y^{\prime},r^{\prime})\phi(y^{\prime\prime}){\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime}{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime}\biggr].

(4.29)

Observe that by substituting
\hat{y}={\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}+y^{\prime}
for
y^{\prime}
and utilizing the even parity of
\phi
from Proposition
3.1
(1
∘
), we have:

\displaystyle I^{\mathds{1}_{[{\varepsilon},\infty)},\mathds{1}_{[0,{\varepsilon})}}_{{\varepsilon},\ref{I22:error}}=I^{\mathds{1}_{[0,{\varepsilon})},\mathds{1}_{[{\varepsilon},\infty)}}_{{\varepsilon},\ref{I22:error}}.

(4.30)

(Alternatively, this identity follows directly from the symmetry of quadratic covariations.) Hence, to obtain the limit of
{\mathbb{E}}[\lvert I^{2,2}_{{\varepsilon}}({g}_{\varepsilon},T)\rvert^{2}]
required in (
4.26
), it suffices to verify the following limits for
(\kappa,\widetilde{\kappa})=(\mathds{1}_{[{\varepsilon},\infty)},\mathds{1}_{[{\varepsilon},\infty)})
,
(\mathds{1}_{[{\varepsilon},\infty)},\mathds{1}_{[0,{\varepsilon})})
, and
(\mathds{1}_{[0,{\varepsilon})},\mathds{1}_{[0,{\varepsilon})})
:

\displaystyle\lim_{{\varepsilon}\to 0}I^{\kappa,\widetilde{\kappa}}_{{\varepsilon},\ref{I22:error}}=0.

(4.31)

The verifications proceed through the next three sub-steps, with the last sub-step giving a conclusion.

Step 2-1.
We verify (
4.31
) in the case of
(\kappa,\widetilde{\kappa})=(\mathds{1}_{[{\varepsilon},\infty)},\mathds{1}_{[{\varepsilon},\infty)})
.
The assumed bound on
g_{\varepsilon}
ensures that
{g}_{\varepsilon}(y,x,s,T)=0
whenever
\lvert y\rvert>M
. Hence, by (
7.3
) with
\delta_{\varepsilon}={\varepsilon}
,

\displaystyle\lvert I^{\mathds{1}_{[{\varepsilon},\infty)},\mathds{1}_{[{\varepsilon},\infty)}}_{{\varepsilon},\ref{I22:error}}\rvert

\displaystyle\quad\leq\int_{r^{\prime}=0}^{T}\int_{y^{\prime}}\int_{y^{\prime\prime}}\int_{z^{\prime\prime}}\int_{\widetilde{z}^{\prime\prime}}\int_{s=r^{\prime}}^{T}\int_{x}\int_{y}\int_{\widetilde{s}=r^{\prime}}^{T}\int_{\widetilde{x}}\int_{\widetilde{y}}\lvert{g}_{\varepsilon}\rvert(y,x,s,T)\lvert{g}_{\varepsilon}\rvert(\widetilde{y},\widetilde{x},\widetilde{s},T)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times C(M){\varepsilon}P_{2(s-r^{\prime})}(x,{\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}+y^{\prime})P_{2(\widetilde{s}-r^{\prime})}(\widetilde{x},y^{\prime})

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times P_{s-r^{\prime}}(x,z^{\prime\prime})P_{\widetilde{s}-r^{\prime}}(\widetilde{x},\widetilde{z}^{\prime\prime})\Lambda_{\varepsilon}{\mathbb{E}}[X_{\varepsilon}(z^{\prime\prime},r^{\prime})X_{\varepsilon}(\widetilde{z}^{\prime\prime},r^{\prime})X_{\varepsilon}({\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}+y^{\prime},r^{\prime})X_{\varepsilon}(y^{\prime},r^{\prime})]\phi(y^{\prime\prime})

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{y}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{x}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}{\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{z}^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}z^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime}{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\int_{r^{\prime}=0}^{T}\int_{y^{\prime}}\int_{y^{\prime\prime}}\int_{s=r^{\prime}}^{T}\int_{x}\int_{y}\int_{\widetilde{s}=r^{\prime}}^{T}\int_{\widetilde{x}}\int_{\widetilde{y}}\lvert{g}_{\varepsilon}\rvert(y,x,s,T)\lvert{g}_{\varepsilon}\rvert(\widetilde{y},\widetilde{x},\widetilde{s},T)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times C(M){\varepsilon}P_{2(s-r^{\prime})}(x,{\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}+y^{\prime})P_{2(\widetilde{s}-r^{\prime})}(\widetilde{x},y^{\prime})

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\frac{C(\lambda,\phi,\lVert X_{0}\rVert_{\infty},T)}{\sqrt{\min\{s,\widetilde{s}\}-r^{\prime}}}\left(\int_{\overline{z}}\phi(\overline{z})\lvert\log\lvert y^{\prime\prime}-\overline{z}\rvert\rvert{\mathrm{d}}\overline{z}+1\right)\phi(y^{\prime\prime})

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{y}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{x}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}{\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime}{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime}.

(4.32)

Here, (
4.32
) can be justified by using Theorem
3.4
(2
∘
) with
p=1/2
after integrating out
\widetilde{z}^{\prime\prime}
and
z^{\prime\prime}
since we can write

\displaystyle\int_{z^{\prime\prime}}\int_{\widetilde{z}^{\prime\prime}}P_{s-r^{\prime}}(x,z^{\prime\prime})P_{\widetilde{s}-r^{\prime}}(\widetilde{x},\widetilde{z}^{\prime\prime})\Lambda_{\varepsilon}{\mathbb{E}}[X_{\varepsilon}(z^{\prime\prime},r^{\prime})X_{\varepsilon}(\widetilde{z}^{\prime\prime},r^{\prime})X_{\varepsilon}({\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}+y^{\prime},r^{\prime})X_{\varepsilon}(y^{\prime},r^{\prime})]{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{z}^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}z^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle\quad=\Lambda_{\varepsilon}{\mathbb{E}}^{(B^{1},B^{2})}_{(x,\widetilde{x})}[X_{\varepsilon}(B^{1}_{s-r^{\prime}},r^{\prime})X_{\varepsilon}(B^{2}_{\widetilde{s}-r^{\prime}},r^{\prime})X_{\varepsilon}({\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}+y^{\prime},r^{\prime})X_{\varepsilon}(y^{\prime},r^{\prime})],

where
B^{1}
and
B^{2}
are independent two-dimensional standard Brownian motions, also independent of
X_{\varepsilon}
.

To use (
4.32
), we consider the effect of integrating out
\widetilde{y}
and
y
therein. After integrating out
y
, we use
(
4.7
) and the assumption on
{g}_{\varepsilon}
to get the following bound:

\displaystyle\int_{y}\lvert{g}_{\varepsilon}\rvert(y,x,s,T){\mathrm{d}}y

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{C(G,m,M)}{1+\lvert x\rvert^{m}}\left[\lvert\log(T-s)\rvert+1+\lvert\log[4(T-s){\varepsilon}^{-2}]\rvert\mathds{1}_{\{4(T-s){\varepsilon}^{-2}<1\}}\right].

(4.33)

An analogous inequality holds with
(y,x,s)
replaced by
(\widetilde{y},\widetilde{x},\widetilde{s})
as we integrate out
\widetilde{y}
.
Hence, by (
4.32
) with
m\geq 10
,

\displaystyle\lvert I^{\mathds{1}_{[{\varepsilon},\infty)},\mathds{1}_{[{\varepsilon},\infty)}}_{{\varepsilon},\ref{I22:error}}\rvert

\displaystyle\leq C(\lambda,\phi,\lVert X_{0}\rVert_{\infty},T,G,m,M)

\displaystyle\quad\times\int_{r^{\prime}=0}^{T}\int_{y^{\prime}}\int_{y^{\prime\prime}}\int_{s=r^{\prime}}^{T}\int_{x}\int_{\widetilde{s}=r^{\prime}}^{T}\int_{\widetilde{x}}\frac{1}{(1+\lvert x\rvert^{m})(1+\lvert\widetilde{x}\rvert^{m})}

\displaystyle\quad\times\left[\lvert\log(T-s)\rvert+1+\lvert\log[4(T-s){\varepsilon}^{-2}]\rvert\mathds{1}_{\{4(T-s){\varepsilon}^{-2}<1\}}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\times\left[\lvert\log(T-\widetilde{s})\rvert+1+\lvert\log[4(T-\widetilde{s}){\varepsilon}^{-2}]\rvert\mathds{1}_{\{4(T-\widetilde{s}){\varepsilon}^{-2}<1\}}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\times{\varepsilon}P_{2(s-r^{\prime})}(x,{\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}+y^{\prime})P_{2(\widetilde{s}-r^{\prime})}(\widetilde{x},y^{\prime})

\displaystyle\quad\times\frac{1}{\sqrt{\min\{s,\widetilde{s}\}-r^{\prime}}}\left(\int_{\overline{z}}\phi(\overline{z})\lvert\log\lvert y^{\prime\prime}-\overline{z}\rvert\rvert{\mathrm{d}}\overline{z}+1\right)\phi(y^{\prime\prime})

\displaystyle\quad\times{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{x}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime}{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime}.

(4.34)

To bound the right-hand side, observe that the integral with respect to
\widetilde{x},x,y^{\prime\prime},y^{\prime}
can be handled as follows, where the first inequality uses (
7.10
):

\displaystyle\int_{y^{\prime}}\int_{y^{\prime\prime}}\int_{x}\int_{\widetilde{x}}\frac{{\varepsilon}P_{2(s-r^{\prime})}(x,{\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}+y^{\prime})P_{2(\widetilde{s}-r^{\prime})}(\widetilde{x},y^{\prime})}{(1+\lvert x\rvert^{m})(1+\lvert\widetilde{x}\rvert^{m})}

\displaystyle\quad\times\left(\int_{\overline{z}}\phi(\overline{z})\lvert\log\lvert y^{\prime\prime}-\overline{z}\rvert\rvert{\mathrm{d}}\overline{z}+1\right)\phi(y^{\prime\prime}){\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{x}{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad\leq C(T,m){\varepsilon}\int_{y^{\prime}}\int_{y^{\prime\prime}}\frac{\phi(y^{\prime\prime})}{(1+\lvert{\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}+y^{\prime}\rvert^{m})(1+\lvert y^{\prime}\rvert^{m})}\left(\int_{\overline{z}}\phi(\overline{z})\lvert\log\lvert y^{\prime\prime}-\overline{z}\rvert\rvert{\mathrm{d}}\overline{z}+1\right){\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad\leq C(\phi,T,m){\varepsilon}.

(4.35)

Note that the last inequality can be seen by using (
7.8
), which yields

(1+\lvert{\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}+y^{\prime}\rvert^{m})^{-1}\leq C(m)(1+\lvert{\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}\rvert^{m})(1+\lvert y^{\prime}\rvert^{m})^{-1}.

Therefore, the bound in (
4.34
) implies the following one:

\displaystyle\lvert I^{\mathds{1}_{[{\varepsilon},\infty)},\mathds{1}_{[{\varepsilon},\infty)}}_{{\varepsilon},\ref{I22:error}}\rvert

\displaystyle\leq C(\lambda,\phi,\lVert X_{0}\rVert_{\infty},T,G,m,M){\varepsilon}\int_{r^{\prime}=0}^{T}\int_{s=r^{\prime}}^{T}\int_{\widetilde{s}=r^{\prime}}^{T}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\min\{s,\widetilde{s}\}-r^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\left[\lvert\log(T-s)\rvert+1+\lvert\log[4(T-s){\varepsilon}^{-2}]\rvert\mathds{1}_{\{4(T-s){\varepsilon}^{-2}<1\}}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\times\left[\lvert\log(T-\widetilde{s})\rvert+1+\lvert\log[4(T-\widetilde{s}){\varepsilon}^{-2}]\rvert\mathds{1}_{\{4(T-\widetilde{s}){\varepsilon}^{-2}<1\}}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}{\mathrm{d}}s{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime}.

(4.36)

We deduce from the last inequality that

\displaystyle\lim_{{\varepsilon}\to 0}I^{\mathds{1}_{[{\varepsilon},\infty)},\mathds{1}_{[{\varepsilon},\infty)}}_{{\varepsilon},\ref{I22:error}}=0,

(4.37)

which is (
4.31
) in the case of
(\kappa,\widetilde{\kappa})=(\mathds{1}_{[{\varepsilon},\infty)},\mathds{1}_{[{\varepsilon},\infty)})
.

Step 2-2.
We verify (
4.31
) in the case of
(\kappa,\widetilde{\kappa})=(\mathds{1}_{[{\varepsilon},\infty)},\mathds{1}_{[0,{\varepsilon})})
. By (
7.3
) with the choice of
\delta_{\varepsilon}={\varepsilon}
, we have the following bound:

\displaystyle\lvert I^{\mathds{1}_{[{\varepsilon},\infty)},\mathds{1}_{(0,{\varepsilon}]}}_{{\varepsilon},\ref{I22:error}}\rvert

\displaystyle\quad\leq\int_{r^{\prime}=0}^{T}\int_{y^{\prime}}\int_{y^{\prime\prime}}\int_{z^{\prime\prime}}\int_{\widetilde{z}^{\prime\prime}}\int_{s=r^{\prime}}^{T}\int_{x}\int_{y}\int_{\widetilde{s}=r^{\prime}}^{T\wedge(r^{\prime}+{\varepsilon})}\int_{\widetilde{x}}\int_{\widetilde{y}}\lvert{g}_{\varepsilon}\rvert(y,x,s,T)\lvert{g}_{\varepsilon}\rvert(\widetilde{y},\widetilde{x},\widetilde{s},T)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times C(M){\varepsilon}^{1/2}P_{2(s-r^{\prime})}(x,{\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}+y^{\prime})[P_{\widetilde{s}-r^{\prime}}({\varepsilon}\widetilde{y}+\widetilde{x},y^{\prime})+P_{\widetilde{s}-r^{\prime}}(\widetilde{x},y^{\prime})]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times P_{s-r^{\prime}}(x,z^{\prime\prime})P_{\widetilde{s}-r^{\prime}}(\widetilde{x},\widetilde{z}^{\prime\prime})\Lambda_{\varepsilon}{\mathbb{E}}[X_{\varepsilon}(z^{\prime\prime},r^{\prime})X_{\varepsilon}(\widetilde{z}^{\prime\prime},r^{\prime})X_{\varepsilon}({\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}+y^{\prime},r^{\prime})X_{\varepsilon}(y^{\prime},r^{\prime})]\phi(y^{\prime\prime})

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{y}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{x}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}{\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{z}^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}z^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime}{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\int_{r^{\prime}=0}^{T}\int_{y^{\prime}}\int_{y^{\prime\prime}}\int_{s=r^{\prime}}^{T}\int_{x}\int_{y}\int_{\widetilde{s}=r^{\prime}}^{T\wedge(r^{\prime}+{\varepsilon})}\int_{\widetilde{x}}\int_{\widetilde{y}}\lvert{g}_{\varepsilon}\rvert(y,x,s,T)\lvert{g}_{\varepsilon}\rvert(\widetilde{y},\widetilde{x},\widetilde{s},T)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times C(M){\varepsilon}^{1/2}P_{2(s-r^{\prime})}(x,{\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}+y^{\prime})[P_{\widetilde{s}-r^{\prime}}({\varepsilon}\widetilde{y}+\widetilde{x},y^{\prime})+P_{\widetilde{s}-r^{\prime}}(\widetilde{x},y^{\prime})]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\frac{C(\lambda,\phi,\lVert X_{0}\rVert_{\infty},T)}{\sqrt{\min\{s,\widetilde{s}\}-r^{\prime}}}\left(\int_{\overline{z}}\phi(\overline{z})\lvert\log\lvert y^{\prime\prime}-\overline{z}\rvert\rvert{\mathrm{d}}\overline{z}+1\right)\phi(y^{\prime\prime})

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{y}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{x}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}{\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime}{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime},

(4.38)

where the last inequality follows by integrating out
\widetilde{z}^{\prime\prime}
and
z^{\prime\prime}
and using Theorem
3.4
(2
∘
) with
p=1/2
.

To estimate the right-hand side of (
4.38
), we first note that the iterated integral over all spatial variables satisfies the first inequality below, which is justified by using the assumed bound for
{g}_{\varepsilon}
with
m\geq 10
:

\displaystyle\int_{y^{\prime}}\int_{y^{\prime\prime}}\int_{x}\int_{y}\int_{\widetilde{x}}\int_{\widetilde{y}}\lvert{g}_{\varepsilon}\rvert(y,x,s,T)\lvert{g}_{\varepsilon}\rvert(\widetilde{y},\widetilde{x},\widetilde{s},T)

\displaystyle\quad\times C(M){\varepsilon}^{1/2}P_{2(s-r^{\prime})}(x,{\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}+y^{\prime})[P_{\widetilde{s}-r^{\prime}}({\varepsilon}\widetilde{y}+\widetilde{x},y^{\prime})+P_{\widetilde{s}-r^{\prime}}(\widetilde{x},y^{\prime})]

\displaystyle\quad\times\left(\int_{\overline{z}}\phi(\overline{z})\lvert\log\lvert y^{\prime\prime}-\overline{z}\rvert\rvert{\mathrm{d}}\overline{z}+1\right)\phi(y^{\prime\prime}){\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{y}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{x}{\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad\leq C(G,m,M)\int_{y^{\prime}}\int_{y^{\prime\prime}}\int_{x}\int_{y}\int_{\widetilde{x}}\int_{\widetilde{y}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\frac{\mathds{1}_{\{\lvert y\rvert\leq M\}}}{1+\lvert x\rvert^{m}}\left[\lvert\log(T-s)\rvert+1+\lvert\log\lvert y\rvert\rvert+{\varepsilon}_{\ref{limT:glue}}\left(\frac{4(T-s){\varepsilon}^{-2}}{\lvert y\rvert^{2}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\frac{\mathds{1}_{\{\lvert\widetilde{y}\rvert\leq M\}}}{1+\lvert\widetilde{x}\rvert^{m}}\left[\lvert\log(T-\widetilde{s})\rvert+1+\lvert\log\lvert\widetilde{y}\rvert\rvert+{\varepsilon}_{\ref{limT:glue}}\left(\frac{4(T-\widetilde{s}){\varepsilon}^{-2}}{\lvert\widetilde{y}\rvert^{2}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times{\varepsilon}^{1/2}P_{2(s-r^{\prime})}(x,{\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}+y^{\prime})[P_{\widetilde{s}-r^{\prime}}({\varepsilon}\widetilde{y}+\widetilde{x},y^{\prime})+P_{\widetilde{s}-r^{\prime}}(\widetilde{x},y^{\prime})]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\left(\int_{\overline{z}}\phi(\overline{z})\lvert\log\lvert y^{\prime\prime}-\overline{z}\rvert\rvert{\mathrm{d}}\overline{z}+1\right)\phi(y^{\prime\prime}){\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{y}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{x}{\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad\leq C(T,G,m,M)\int_{y^{\prime}}\int_{y^{\prime\prime}}\int_{y}\int_{\widetilde{y}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\mathds{1}_{\{\lvert y\rvert\leq M\}}\left[\lvert\log(T-s)\rvert+1+\lvert\log\lvert y\rvert\rvert+{\varepsilon}_{\ref{limT:glue}}\left(\frac{4(T-s){\varepsilon}^{-2}}{\lvert y\rvert^{2}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\mathds{1}_{\{\lvert\widetilde{y}\rvert\leq M\}}\left[\lvert\log(T-\widetilde{s})\rvert+1+\lvert\log\lvert\widetilde{y}\rvert\rvert+{\varepsilon}_{\ref{limT:glue}}\left(\frac{4(T-\widetilde{s}){\varepsilon}^{-2}}{\lvert\widetilde{y}\rvert^{2}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times{\varepsilon}^{1/2}\left(\frac{1+\lvert{\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}\rvert^{m}}{1+\lvert y^{\prime}\rvert^{m}}\frac{1+\lvert{\varepsilon}\widetilde{y}\rvert^{m}}{1+\lvert y^{\prime}\rvert^{m}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\left(\int_{\overline{z}}\phi(\overline{z})\lvert\log\lvert y^{\prime\prime}-\overline{z}\rvert\rvert{\mathrm{d}}\overline{z}+1\right)\phi(y^{\prime\prime}){\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{y}{\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime},

where the last inequality follows by integrating out
\widetilde{x}
and
x
and using (
7.8
) and (
7.10
).
Also, by integrating out
y^{\prime}
,
y^{\prime\prime}
,
y
and
\widetilde{y}
in the same order and using (
4.7
), the last inequality implies

\displaystyle\int_{y^{\prime}}\int_{y^{\prime\prime}}\int_{x}\int_{y}\int_{\widetilde{x}}\int_{\widetilde{y}}\lvert{g}_{\varepsilon}\rvert(y,x,s,T)\lvert{g}_{\varepsilon}\rvert(\widetilde{y},\widetilde{x},\widetilde{s},T)

\displaystyle\quad\times C(M){\varepsilon}^{1/2}P_{2(s-r^{\prime})}(x,{\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}+y^{\prime})[P_{\widetilde{s}-r^{\prime}}({\varepsilon}\widetilde{y}+\widetilde{x},y^{\prime})+P_{\widetilde{s}-r^{\prime}}(\widetilde{x},y^{\prime})]

\displaystyle\quad\times\left(\int_{\overline{z}}\phi(\overline{z})\lvert\log\lvert y^{\prime\prime}-\overline{z}\rvert\rvert{\mathrm{d}}\overline{z}+1\right)\phi(y^{\prime\prime}){\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{y}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{x}{\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad\leq C(\phi,T,G,m,M){\varepsilon}^{1/2}\left[\lvert\log(T-s)\rvert+1+\lvert\log[4(T-s){\varepsilon}^{-2}]\rvert\mathds{1}_{\{4(T-s){\varepsilon}^{-2}<1\}}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\left[\lvert\log(T-\widetilde{s})\rvert+1+\lvert\log[4(T-\widetilde{s}){\varepsilon}^{-2}]\rvert\mathds{1}_{\{4(T-\widetilde{s}){\varepsilon}^{-2}<1\}}\right].

Applying the right-hand side to the right-hand side of (
4.38
), we get

\displaystyle\lvert I^{\mathds{1}_{[{\varepsilon},\infty)},\mathds{1}_{(0,{\varepsilon}]}}_{\ref{I22:error}}\rvert

\displaystyle\leq C(\lambda,\phi,\lVert X_{0}\rVert_{\infty},T,G,m,M){\varepsilon}^{1/2}\int_{r^{\prime}=0}^{T}\int_{s=r^{\prime}}^{T}\int_{\widetilde{s}=r^{\prime}}^{T\wedge(r^{\prime}+{\varepsilon})}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\min\{s,\widetilde{s}\}-r^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\left[\lvert\log(T-s)\rvert+1+\lvert\log[4(T-s){\varepsilon}^{-2}]\rvert\mathds{1}_{\{4(T-s){\varepsilon}^{-2}<1\}}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\times\left[\lvert\log(T-\widetilde{s})\rvert+1+\lvert\log[4(T-\widetilde{s}){\varepsilon}^{-2}]\rvert\mathds{1}_{\{4(T-\widetilde{s}){\varepsilon}^{-2}<1\}}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}{\mathrm{d}}s{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime}.

It follows that

\displaystyle\lim_{{\varepsilon}\to 0}I^{\mathds{1}_{[{\varepsilon},\infty)},\mathds{1}_{(0,{\varepsilon}]}}_{\ref{I22:error}}=0,

(4.39)

which is (
4.31
) in the case of
(\kappa,\widetilde{\kappa})=(\mathds{1}_{[{\varepsilon},\infty)},\mathds{1}_{[0,{\varepsilon})})
.

Step 2-3.
We now address (
4.31
) in the final case, where
(\kappa,\widetilde{\kappa})=(\mathds{1}_{[0,{\varepsilon})},\mathds{1}_{[0,{\varepsilon})})
. In this situation, we have only the following straightforward bound available:

\displaystyle\lvert I^{\mathds{1}_{(0,{\varepsilon}]},\mathds{1}_{(0,{\varepsilon}]}}_{\ref{I22:error}}\rvert

\displaystyle\quad\leq\int_{r^{\prime}=0}^{T}\int_{y^{\prime}}\int_{y^{\prime\prime}}\int_{z^{\prime\prime}}\int_{\widetilde{z}^{\prime\prime}}\int_{s=r^{\prime}}^{T\wedge(r^{\prime}+{\varepsilon})}\int_{x}\int_{y}\int_{\widetilde{s}=r^{\prime}}^{T\wedge(r^{\prime}+{\varepsilon})}\int_{\widetilde{x}}\int_{\widetilde{y}}\lvert{g}_{\varepsilon}\rvert(y,x,s,T)\lvert{g}_{\varepsilon}\rvert(\widetilde{y},\widetilde{x},\widetilde{s},T)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times[P_{s-r^{\prime}}({\varepsilon}y+x,{\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}+y^{\prime})+P_{s-r^{\prime}}(x,{\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}+y^{\prime})][P_{\widetilde{s}-r^{\prime}}({\varepsilon}\widetilde{y}+\widetilde{x},y^{\prime})+P_{\widetilde{s}-r^{\prime}}(\widetilde{x},y^{\prime})]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times P_{s-r^{\prime}}(x,z^{\prime\prime})P_{\widetilde{s}-r^{\prime}}(\widetilde{x},\widetilde{z}^{\prime\prime})\Lambda_{\varepsilon}{\mathbb{E}}[X_{\varepsilon}(z^{\prime\prime},r^{\prime})X_{\varepsilon}(\widetilde{z}^{\prime\prime},r^{\prime})X_{\varepsilon}({\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}+y^{\prime},r^{\prime})X_{\varepsilon}(y^{\prime},r^{\prime})]\phi(y^{\prime\prime})

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{y}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{x}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}{\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{z}^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}z^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime}{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\int_{r^{\prime}=0}^{T}\int_{y^{\prime}}\int_{y^{\prime\prime}}\int_{s=r^{\prime}}^{T\wedge(r^{\prime}+{\varepsilon})}\int_{x}\int_{y}\int_{\widetilde{s}=r^{\prime}}^{T\wedge(r^{\prime}+{\varepsilon})}\int_{\widetilde{x}}\int_{\widetilde{y}}\lvert{g}_{\varepsilon}\rvert(y,x,s,T)\lvert{g}_{\varepsilon}\rvert(\widetilde{y},\widetilde{x},\widetilde{s},T)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times[P_{s-r^{\prime}}({\varepsilon}y+x,{\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}+y^{\prime})+P_{s-r^{\prime}}(x,{\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}+y^{\prime})][P_{\widetilde{s}-r^{\prime}}({\varepsilon}\widetilde{y}+\widetilde{x},y^{\prime})+P_{\widetilde{s}-r^{\prime}}(\widetilde{x},y^{\prime})]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\frac{C(\lambda,\phi,\lVert X_{0}\rVert_{\infty},T)}{\sqrt{\min\{s,\widetilde{s}\}-r^{\prime}}}\left(\int_{\overline{z}}\phi(\overline{z})\lvert\log\lvert y^{\prime\prime}-\overline{z}\rvert\rvert{\mathrm{d}}\overline{z}+1\right)\phi(y^{\prime\prime})

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{y}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{x}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}{\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime}{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime}.

(4.40)

Here, the last inequality is justified by integrating out
\widetilde{z}^{\prime\prime}
and
z^{\prime\prime}
and then using
Theorem
3.4
(2
∘
) with
p=1/2
.

To integrate out the remaining spatial variables on the right-hand side of (
4.40
), we isolate the relevant multiple integral and use the assumed bound on
{g}_{\varepsilon}
for
m\geq 10
. This yields

\displaystyle\int_{y^{\prime}}\int_{y^{\prime\prime}}\int_{x}\int_{y}\int_{\widetilde{x}}\int_{\widetilde{y}}\lvert{g}_{\varepsilon}\rvert(y,x,s,T)\lvert{g}_{\varepsilon}\rvert(\widetilde{y},\widetilde{x},\widetilde{s},T)

\displaystyle\quad\times[P_{s-r^{\prime}}({\varepsilon}y+x,{\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}+y^{\prime})+P_{s-r^{\prime}}(x,{\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}+y^{\prime})][P_{\widetilde{s}-r^{\prime}}({\varepsilon}\widetilde{y}+\widetilde{x},y^{\prime})+P_{\widetilde{s}-r^{\prime}}(\widetilde{x},y^{\prime})]

\displaystyle\quad\times\left(\int_{\overline{z}}\phi(\overline{z})\lvert\log\lvert y^{\prime\prime}-\overline{z}\rvert\rvert{\mathrm{d}}\overline{z}+1\right)\phi(y^{\prime\prime}){\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{y}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{x}{\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad\leq C(G,m)\int_{y^{\prime}}\int_{y^{\prime\prime}}\int_{x}\int_{y}\int_{\widetilde{x}}\int_{\widetilde{y}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\frac{\mathds{1}_{\{\lvert y\rvert\leq M\}}}{1+\lvert x\rvert^{m}}\left[\lvert\log(T-s)\rvert+1+\lvert\log\lvert y\rvert\rvert+{\varepsilon}_{\ref{limT:glue}}\left(\frac{4(T-s){\varepsilon}^{-2}}{\lvert y\rvert^{2}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\frac{\mathds{1}_{\{\lvert\widetilde{y}\rvert\leq M\}}}{1+\lvert\widetilde{x}\rvert^{m}}\left[\lvert\log(T-\widetilde{s})\rvert+1+\lvert\log\lvert\widetilde{y}\rvert\rvert+{\varepsilon}_{\ref{limT:glue}}\left(\frac{4(T-\widetilde{s}){\varepsilon}^{-2}}{\lvert\widetilde{y}\rvert^{2}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times[P_{s-r^{\prime}}({\varepsilon}y+x,{\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}+y^{\prime})+P_{s-r^{\prime}}(x,{\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}+y^{\prime})][P_{\widetilde{s}-r^{\prime}}({\varepsilon}\widetilde{y}+\widetilde{x},y^{\prime})+P_{\widetilde{s}-r^{\prime}}(\widetilde{x},y^{\prime})]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\left(\int_{\overline{z}}\phi(\overline{z})\lvert\log\lvert y^{\prime\prime}-\overline{z}\rvert\rvert{\mathrm{d}}\overline{z}+1\right)\phi(y^{\prime\prime}){\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{y}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{x}{\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad\leq C(T,G,m)\int_{y^{\prime}}\int_{y^{\prime\prime}}\int_{y}\int_{\widetilde{y}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\mathds{1}_{\{\lvert y\rvert\leq M\}}\left[\lvert\log(T-s)\rvert+1+\lvert\log\lvert y\rvert\rvert+{\varepsilon}_{\ref{limT:glue}}\left(\frac{4(T-s){\varepsilon}^{-2}}{\lvert y\rvert^{2}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\mathds{1}_{\{\lvert\widetilde{y}\rvert\leq M\}}\left[\lvert\log(T-\widetilde{s})\rvert+1+\lvert\log\lvert\widetilde{y}\rvert\rvert+{\varepsilon}_{\ref{limT:glue}}\left(\frac{4(T-\widetilde{s}){\varepsilon}^{-2}}{\lvert\widetilde{y}\rvert^{2}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\left(\frac{1+\lvert{\varepsilon}y\rvert^{m}+\lvert{\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}\rvert^{m}}{1+\lvert y^{\prime}\rvert^{m}}\frac{1+\lvert{\varepsilon}\widetilde{y}\rvert^{m}}{1+\lvert y^{\prime}\rvert^{m}}\right)\left(\int_{\overline{z}}\phi(\overline{z})\lvert\log\lvert y^{\prime\prime}-\overline{z}\rvert\rvert{\mathrm{d}}\overline{z}+1\right)\phi(y^{\prime\prime}){\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{y}{\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime}.

Here, the last inequality is obtained by integrating out
x
and
\widetilde{x}
, and applying the following: the inequalities in (
7.8
) and (
7.10
), along with
(a+b)^{m}\leq C(m)(a^{m}+b^{m})
for all
a,b\geq 0
. Furthermore, by integrating out
y^{\prime\prime}
,
y^{\prime}
,
y
and
\widetilde{y}
in the same order and using (
4.7
), the last inequality gives

\displaystyle\int_{y^{\prime}}\int_{y^{\prime\prime}}\int_{x}\int_{y}\int_{\widetilde{x}}\int_{\widetilde{y}}\lvert{g}_{\varepsilon}\rvert(y,x,s,T)\lvert{g}_{\varepsilon}\rvert(\widetilde{y},\widetilde{x},\widetilde{s},T)

\displaystyle\quad\times[P_{s-r^{\prime}}({\varepsilon}y+x,{\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}+y^{\prime})+P_{s-r^{\prime}}(x,{\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime}+y^{\prime})][P_{\widetilde{s}-r^{\prime}}({\varepsilon}\widetilde{y}+\widetilde{x},y^{\prime})+P_{\widetilde{s}-r^{\prime}}(\widetilde{x},y^{\prime})]

\displaystyle\quad\times\left(\int_{\overline{z}}\phi(\overline{z})\lvert\log\lvert y^{\prime\prime}-\overline{z}\rvert\rvert{\mathrm{d}}\overline{z}+1\right)\phi(y^{\prime\prime}){\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{y}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{x}{\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad\leq C(\phi,T,G,m,M)\left[\lvert\log(T-s)\rvert+1+\lvert\log[4(T-s){\varepsilon}^{-2}]\rvert\mathds{1}_{\{4(T-s){\varepsilon}^{-2}<1\}}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\left[\lvert\log(T-\widetilde{s})\rvert+1+\lvert\log[4(T-\widetilde{s}){\varepsilon}^{-2}]\rvert\mathds{1}_{\{4(T-\widetilde{s}){\varepsilon}^{-2}<1\}}\right].

Finally, applying the right-hand side of the foregoing inequality to the right-hand side of (
4.40
), we get

\displaystyle\lvert I^{\mathds{1}_{(0,{\varepsilon}]},\mathds{1}_{(0,{\varepsilon}]}}_{{\varepsilon},\ref{I22:error}}\rvert

\displaystyle\leq C(\lambda,\phi,\lVert X_{0}\rVert_{\infty},T,G,m,M)\int_{r^{\prime}=0}^{T}\int_{s=r^{\prime}}^{T\wedge(r^{\prime}+{\varepsilon})}\int_{\widetilde{s}=r^{\prime}}^{T\wedge(r^{\prime}+{\varepsilon})}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\min\{s,\widetilde{s}\}-r^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\left[\lvert\log(T-s)\rvert+1+\lvert\log[4(T-s){\varepsilon}^{-2}]\rvert\mathds{1}_{\{4(T-s){\varepsilon}^{-2}<1\}}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\times\left[\lvert\log(T-\widetilde{s})\rvert+1+\lvert\log[4(T-\widetilde{s}){\varepsilon}^{-2}]\rvert\mathds{1}_{\{4(T-\widetilde{s}){\varepsilon}^{-2}<1\}}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}{\mathrm{d}}s{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime}.

It follows that

\displaystyle\lim_{{\varepsilon}\to 0}I^{\mathds{1}_{(0,{\varepsilon}]},\mathds{1}_{(0,{\varepsilon}]}}_{{\varepsilon},\ref{I22:error}}=0,

(4.41)

which is (
4.31
) in the case of
(\kappa,\widetilde{\kappa})=(\mathds{1}_{[0,{\varepsilon})},\mathds{1}_{[0,{\varepsilon})})
.

Step 2-4.

) and (

). Hence, combining (

Step 3.
Observe that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\lvert I^{2,3}_{{\varepsilon}}({g}_{\varepsilon},T)\rvert^{2}]=I^{\mathds{1},\mathds{1}}_{{\varepsilon},\ref{I23:error}}=\sum\left\{I^{\kappa,\widetilde{\kappa}}_{{\varepsilon},\ref{I23:error}};\kappa,\widetilde{\kappa}\in\{\mathds{1}_{[0,{\varepsilon})},\mathds{1}_{[{\varepsilon},\infty)}\}\right\},

where

\displaystyle\quad\times\bigg(\int_{z^{\prime}}\int_{s=r^{\prime\prime}}^{T}\kappa(s-r^{\prime\prime})\int_{x}\int_{y}{g}_{\varepsilon}(y,x,s,T)[P_{s-r^{\prime\prime}}({\varepsilon}y+x,z^{\prime})-P_{s-r^{\prime\prime}}(x,z^{\prime})]

In this case, we still have

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{y}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{x}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}{\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{z}^{\prime}{\mathrm{d}}z^{\prime}{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\int_{r^{\prime\prime}=0}^{T}\int_{y^{\prime\prime}}\int_{y^{\prime\prime\prime}}\int_{s=r^{\prime\prime}}^{T}\int_{x}\int_{y}\int_{\widetilde{s}=r^{\prime\prime}}^{T}\int_{\widetilde{x}}\int_{\widetilde{y}}\lvert{g}_{\varepsilon}\rvert(y,x,s,T)\lvert{g}_{\varepsilon}\rvert(\widetilde{y},\widetilde{x},\widetilde{s},T)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times C(M){\varepsilon}P_{s-r^{\prime\prime}}(x,{\varepsilon}y^{\prime\prime\prime}+y^{\prime\prime})P_{\widetilde{s}-r^{\prime\prime}}(\widetilde{x},y^{\prime\prime})

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\frac{C(\lambda,\phi,\lVert X_{0}\rVert_{\infty},T)}{\sqrt{\min\{s,\widetilde{s}\}-r^{\prime\prime}}}\left(\int_{\overline{z}}\phi(\overline{z})\lvert\log\lvert y^{\prime\prime\prime}-\overline{z}\rvert\rvert{\mathrm{d}}\overline{z}+1\right)\phi(y^{\prime\prime\prime})

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{y}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{x}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}{\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime\prime},

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{y}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{x}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}{\mathrm{d}}y{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}s{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}y^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}r^{\prime\prime},

Step 3-3.

Step 4.
To prove that the limit superior of
