(\widetilde{x}_{2}^{2},t^{\prime}-t)=(x_{0}^{4},0)
. Figure
4
illustrates
\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}_{\varepsilon}^{\widetilde{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}}\oplus\mathcal{G}_{\varepsilon}^{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}
in this scenario. Moreover, this extension of paths can be performed using the Chapman–Kolmogorov equation when writing out
\int{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}^{\widetilde{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}}_{{\varepsilon};0}\int{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}^{\widetilde{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}}_{{\varepsilon};1}\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};0}\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};1}\cdots\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};m}\mathds{1}
. The remainder of this iterated graphical integral is then handled as follows:

•

Expand the remainder of
\int{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}^{\widetilde{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}}_{{\varepsilon};0}\int{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}^{\widetilde{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}}_{{\varepsilon};1}
as in (
6.38
) and (
6.39
).

•

For
m=1
, apply (
6.54
). For
m\geq 2
, utilize (
6.55
) with
\ell_{0}=1
to bound
\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};1}\cdots\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};m}\mathds{1}
.

Hence, we obtain the inequality below, which also handles the cases of
\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}=1
and
\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}=0
simultaneously:

\displaystyle\int{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}^{\widetilde{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}}_{{\varepsilon};0}\int{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}^{\widetilde{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}}_{{\varepsilon};1}\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};0}\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};1}\cdots\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};m}\mathds{1}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{4}}\begin{bmatrix}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{x}_{1}^{2}\\
{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{x}_{1}^{1}\end{bmatrix}_{\otimes}\varphi(\widetilde{x}_{1}^{\mathbf{i}_{0}})\begin{bmatrix}P_{\widetilde{s}_{1}}(\widetilde{x}_{0}^{2},\widetilde{x}_{1}^{1}+{\varepsilon}{\sqrt{2}}\widetilde{x}^{\mathbf{i}_{0}}_{1})\\
P_{\widetilde{s}_{1}}(\widetilde{x}_{0}^{1},\widetilde{x}_{1}^{1})\end{bmatrix}_{\times}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{4}}\begin{bmatrix}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{z}_{1}^{\prime}\\
{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{z}_{1}\end{bmatrix}_{\otimes}\varphi(\widetilde{z}_{1})\begin{bmatrix}P_{\widetilde{s}_{1},\widetilde{\tau}_{1}}({\sqrt{2}}\widetilde{x}_{1}^{1}+{\varepsilon}\widetilde{x}_{1}^{\mathbf{i}_{0}},\widetilde{z}^{\prime}_{1})\\
\vskip-8.5359pt\\
{\mathfrak{s}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(\widetilde{s}_{1},\widetilde{\tau}_{1};\widetilde{x}_{1}^{\mathbf{i}_{0}},\widetilde{z}_{1})\end{bmatrix}_{\times}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{4}}\begin{bmatrix}{\mathrm{d}}x^{4}_{1}\\
{\mathrm{d}}x^{3}_{1}\end{bmatrix}_{\otimes}\varphi(x^{\mathbf{i}_{1}}_{1})\begin{bmatrix}P_{t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{\tau}_{1}+u_{1}}\left(\frac{\widetilde{z}_{1}^{\prime}+{\varepsilon}\widetilde{z}_{1}}{{\sqrt{2}}},x_{1}^{3}+{\varepsilon}{\sqrt{2}}x^{\mathbf{i}_{1}}_{1}\right)\\
P_{t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{\tau}_{1}+u_{1}}\left(\frac{\widetilde{z}_{1}^{\prime}-{\varepsilon}\widetilde{z}_{1}}{{\sqrt{2}}},x_{1}^{3}\right)\end{bmatrix}_{\times}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\Biggl(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}z_{1}{\mathfrak{s}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(0,v_{1};x_{1}^{\mathbf{i}_{1}},z_{1})\varphi(z_{1})\sigma_{1}+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}\Biggr)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\Biggl(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=1\atop\scriptstyle 2\leq\ell\leq m}\overline{{\mathfrak{s}}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(v_{\ell})\Biggr)(\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{1/2})^{\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\#_{\ell}}\left(\prod_{\ell=1}^{m-1}\frac{1}{2u_{\ell+1}+u_{\ell}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\widehat{\widetilde{\sigma}}_{1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{4}}\begin{bmatrix}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{x}_{1}^{2}\\
{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{x}_{1}^{1}\end{bmatrix}_{\otimes}\varphi(\widetilde{x}_{1}^{\mathbf{i}_{0}})\begin{bmatrix}P_{\widetilde{s}_{1}}(\widetilde{x}_{0}^{2},\widetilde{x}_{1}^{1}+{\varepsilon}{\sqrt{2}}\widetilde{x}^{\mathbf{i}_{0}}_{1})\\
P_{\widetilde{s}_{1}}(\widetilde{x}_{0}^{1},\widetilde{x}_{1}^{1})\end{bmatrix}_{\times}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{4}}\begin{bmatrix}{\mathrm{d}}x^{4}_{1}\\
{\mathrm{d}}x^{3}_{1}\end{bmatrix}_{\otimes}\varphi(x^{\mathbf{i}_{1}}_{1})\begin{bmatrix}\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{1/2}P_{t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{\tau}_{1}+u_{1}}(\widetilde{x}_{1}^{1}+{\varepsilon}{\sqrt{2}}\widetilde{x}_{1}^{\mathbf{i}_{0}},x_{1}^{3}+{\varepsilon}{\sqrt{2}}x^{\mathbf{i}_{1}}_{1})\\
\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{1/2}P_{t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{\tau}_{1}+u_{1}}(\widetilde{x}_{1}^{1},x_{1}^{3})\end{bmatrix}_{\times}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\Biggl(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}z_{1}{\mathfrak{s}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(0,v_{1};x_{1}^{\mathbf{i}_{1}},z_{1})\varphi(z_{1})\sigma_{1}+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}\Biggr)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\Biggl(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=1\atop\scriptstyle 2\leq\ell\leq m}\overline{{\mathfrak{s}}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(v_{\ell})\Biggr)(\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{1/2})^{\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\#_{\ell}}\left(\prod_{\ell=1}^{m-1}\frac{1}{2u_{\ell+1}+u_{\ell}}\right).

(6.65)

To simplify the right-hand side of (
6.65
), we apply (
6.53
) multiple times, starting with the integrals with respect to
{\mathrm{d}}x_{1}^{4}\otimes{\mathrm{d}}x_{1}^{3}
, and use the identity
\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\#_{\ell}=2\lVert\widehat{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}\rVert
from (
6.64
). Hence,

\displaystyle\int{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}^{\widetilde{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}}_{{\varepsilon};0}\int{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}^{\widetilde{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}}_{{\varepsilon};1}\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};0}\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};1}\cdots\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};m}\mathds{1}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\overline{z}}\phi(\widetilde{\overline{z}})P_{2\widetilde{s}_{1}}(\widetilde{x}_{0}^{2}-{\varepsilon}\widetilde{\overline{z}},\widetilde{x}_{0}^{1})\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{z}_{1}\varphi(\widetilde{z}_{1}){\mathfrak{s}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(\widetilde{s}_{1},\widetilde{\tau}_{1};\widetilde{\overline{z}}/{\sqrt{2}},\widetilde{z}_{1})

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\overline{z}\phi(\overline{z})P_{2(t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{\tau}_{1}+u_{1})}({\varepsilon}{\sqrt{2}}\widetilde{z}_{1}-{\varepsilon}\overline{z})\Biggl(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}z_{1}{\mathfrak{s}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(0,v_{1};\overline{z}/{\sqrt{2}},z_{1})\varphi(z_{1})\sigma_{1}+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}\Biggr)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\Biggl(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=1\atop\scriptstyle 2\leq\ell\leq m}\overline{{\mathfrak{s}}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(v_{\ell})\Biggr)\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{\lVert\widehat{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}\rVert}\left(\prod_{\ell=1}^{m-1}\frac{1}{2u_{\ell+1}+u_{\ell}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\widehat{\widetilde{\sigma}}_{1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\overline{z}}\phi(\widetilde{\overline{z}})P_{2\widetilde{s}_{1}}(\widetilde{x}_{0}^{2}-{\varepsilon}\widetilde{\overline{z}},\widetilde{x}_{0}^{1})

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\overline{z}\phi(\overline{z})P_{2(t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{\tau}_{1}+u_{1})}({\varepsilon}\widetilde{\overline{z}}-{\varepsilon}\overline{z})\Biggl(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}z_{1}{\mathfrak{s}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(0,v_{1};\overline{z}/{\sqrt{2}},z_{1})\varphi(z_{1})\sigma_{1}+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}\Biggr)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\Biggl(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=1\atop\scriptstyle 2\leq\ell\leq m}\overline{{\mathfrak{s}}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(v_{\ell})\Biggr)\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{1+\lVert\widehat{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}\rVert}\left(\prod_{\ell=1}^{m-1}\frac{1}{2u_{\ell+1}+u_{\ell}}\right).

(6.66)

We now obtain (
6.51
) for
\mathbf{i}_{1}=(4,3)
and
m\geq 1
upon applying the bound

\max\{P_{2(t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{\tau}_{1}+u_{1})}({\varepsilon}{\sqrt{2}}\widetilde{z}_{1}-{\varepsilon}\overline{z}),P_{2(t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{\tau}_{1}+u_{1})}({\varepsilon}\widetilde{\overline{z}}-{\varepsilon}\overline{z})\}\leq(t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{\tau}_{1}+u_{1})^{-1}

and the definition (
6.45
) of
\overline{{\mathfrak{s}}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(\cdot)

to the right-hand side of (
6.66
).

To establish (
6.52
) for
\mathbf{i}=(4,3)
and
m\geq 1
, we use the following inequality, which results from a modification of the derivation of (
6.65
):

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}^{(\widetilde{B}^{1},\widetilde{B}^{2})}_{(\widetilde{x}_{0}^{1},\widetilde{x}_{0}^{2})}\Bigg[\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};0}\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};1}\cdots\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};m}\mathds{1}\mid_{(x_{0}^{3},x_{0}^{4})=(\widetilde{B}^{1}_{t^{\prime}-t},\widetilde{B}^{2}_{t^{\prime}-t})}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\quad\leq\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{4}}\begin{bmatrix}{\mathrm{d}}x^{4}_{1}\\
{\mathrm{d}}x^{3}_{1}\end{bmatrix}_{\otimes}\varphi(x^{\mathbf{i}_{1}}_{1})\begin{bmatrix}P_{t^{\prime}-t+u_{1}}(\widetilde{x}_{0}^{2},x_{1}^{3}+{\varepsilon}{\sqrt{2}}x^{\mathbf{i}_{1}}_{1})\\
P_{t^{\prime}-t+u_{1}}(\widetilde{x}_{0}^{1},x_{1}^{3})\end{bmatrix}_{\times}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\Biggl(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}z_{1}{\mathfrak{s}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(0,v_{1};x_{1}^{\mathbf{i}_{1}},z_{1})\varphi(z_{1})\sigma_{1}+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}\Biggr)\Biggl(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=1\atop\scriptstyle 2\leq\ell\leq m}\overline{{\mathfrak{s}}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(v_{\ell})\Biggr)\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{\lVert\widehat{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}\rVert}\left(\prod_{\ell=1}^{m-1}\frac{1}{2u_{\ell+1}+u_{\ell}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\overline{z}\phi(\overline{z})P_{2(t^{\prime}-t+u_{1})}(\widetilde{x}_{0}^{2}-{\varepsilon}\overline{z},\widetilde{x}_{0}^{1})\Biggl(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}z_{1}{\mathfrak{s}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(0,v_{1};\overline{z}/{\sqrt{2}},z_{1})\varphi(z_{1})\sigma_{1}+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}\Biggr)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\Biggl(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=1\atop\scriptstyle 2\leq\ell\leq m}\overline{{\mathfrak{s}}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(v_{\ell})\Biggr)\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{\lVert\widehat{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}\rVert}\left(\prod_{\ell=1}^{m-1}\frac{1}{2u_{\ell+1}+u_{\ell}}\right).

Accordingly, we can apply a similar argument to that used below (
6.66
) to derive (
6.52
) for
\mathbf{i}=(4,3)
and
m\geq 1
. Thus, all the required inequalities for
\mathbf{i}=(4,3)
have been established.

Step 5.
To prove (
6.51
) for
\mathbf{i}_{1}=(3,2)
and
m\geq 1
, we use the observation that exactly one of the two edges defining
\mathcal{G}^{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}_{{\varepsilon};0}
can be extended to a left-maximal entanglement-free path in
\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}_{\varepsilon}^{\widetilde{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}}\oplus\mathcal{G}_{\varepsilon}^{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}
, and the duration of this edge increases from
u_{1}=s_{1}
to
t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{\tau}_{1}+u_{1}
. Moreover, that edge in
\mathcal{G}^{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}_{{\varepsilon};0}
must be the one whose left-end vertex is
(\widetilde{x}^{1}_{2},t^{\prime}-t)=(x_{0}^{3},0)
, as illustrated in the two examples of Figure
5
. We will not use the other extension via
(\widetilde{x}^{2}_{2},t^{\prime}-t)=(x_{0}^{4},0)
, though.

By using the above observation, the first bound for
\int{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}^{\widetilde{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}}_{{\varepsilon};0}\int{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}^{\widetilde{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}}_{{\varepsilon};1}\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};0}\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};1}\cdots\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};m}\mathds{1}
can be obtained similarly as in (
6.65
). Thus, we have the following:

\displaystyle\int{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}^{\widetilde{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}}_{{\varepsilon};0}\int{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}^{\widetilde{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}}_{{\varepsilon};1}\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};0}\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};1}\cdots\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};m}\mathds{1}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{4}}\begin{bmatrix}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{x}_{1}^{2}\\
{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{x}_{1}^{1}\end{bmatrix}_{\otimes}\varphi(\widetilde{x}_{1}^{\mathbf{i}_{0}})\begin{bmatrix}P_{\widetilde{s}_{1}}(\widetilde{x}_{0}^{2},\widetilde{x}_{1}^{1}+{\varepsilon}{\sqrt{2}}\widetilde{x}^{\mathbf{i}_{0}}_{1})\\
P_{\widetilde{s}_{1}}(\widetilde{x}_{0}^{1},\widetilde{x}_{1}^{1})\end{bmatrix}_{\times}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{4}}\begin{bmatrix}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{z}_{1}^{\prime}\\
{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{z}_{1}\end{bmatrix}_{\otimes}\varphi(\widetilde{z}_{1})\begin{bmatrix}P_{\widetilde{s}_{1},\widetilde{\tau}_{1}}({\sqrt{2}}\widetilde{x}_{1}^{1}+{\varepsilon}\widetilde{x}_{1}^{\mathbf{i}_{0}},\widetilde{z}^{\prime}_{1})\\
\vskip-8.5359pt\\
{\mathfrak{s}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(\widetilde{s}_{1},\widetilde{\tau}_{1};\widetilde{x}_{1}^{\mathbf{i}_{0}},\widetilde{z}_{1})\end{bmatrix}_{\times}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{4}}\begin{bmatrix}{\mathrm{d}}x^{3}_{1}\\
{\mathrm{d}}x^{2}_{1}\end{bmatrix}_{\otimes}\varphi(x^{\mathbf{i}_{1}}_{1})\begin{bmatrix}P_{t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{\tau}_{1}+u_{1}}\left(\frac{\widetilde{z}_{1}^{\prime}-{\varepsilon}\widetilde{z}_{1}}{{\sqrt{2}}},x_{1}^{2}+{\varepsilon}{\sqrt{2}}x_{1}^{\mathbf{i}_{1}}\right)\\
P_{u_{1}}(x_{0}^{2},x_{1}^{2})\end{bmatrix}_{\times}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\Biggl(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}z_{1}{\mathfrak{s}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(0,v_{1};x_{1}^{\mathbf{i}_{1}},z_{1})\varphi(z_{1})\sigma_{1}+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}\Biggr)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\Biggl(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=1\atop\scriptstyle 2\leq\ell\leq m}\overline{{\mathfrak{s}}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(v_{\ell})\Biggr)(\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{1/2})^{\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\#_{\ell}}\left(\prod_{\ell=1}^{m-1}\frac{1}{2u_{\ell+1}+u_{\ell}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\widehat{\widetilde{\sigma}}_{1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{4}}\begin{bmatrix}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{x}_{1}^{2}\\
{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{x}_{1}^{1}\end{bmatrix}_{\otimes}\varphi(\widetilde{x}_{1}^{\mathbf{i}_{0}})\begin{bmatrix}P_{\widetilde{s}_{1}}(\widetilde{x}_{0}^{2},\widetilde{x}_{1}^{1}+{\varepsilon}{\sqrt{2}}\widetilde{x}^{\mathbf{i}_{0}}_{1})\\
P_{\widetilde{s}_{1}}(\widetilde{x}_{0}^{1},\widetilde{x}_{1}^{1})\end{bmatrix}_{\times}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{4}}\begin{bmatrix}{\mathrm{d}}x^{3}_{1}\\
{\mathrm{d}}x^{2}_{1}\end{bmatrix}_{\otimes}\varphi(x^{\mathbf{i}_{1}}_{1})\begin{bmatrix}\Lambda_{\varepsilon}P_{t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{\tau}_{1}+u_{1}}(\widetilde{x}_{1}^{1},x_{1}^{2}+{\varepsilon}{\sqrt{2}}x_{1}^{\mathbf{i}_{1}})\\
P_{u_{1}}(x_{0}^{2},x_{1}^{2})\end{bmatrix}_{\times}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\Biggl(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}z_{1}{\mathfrak{s}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(0,v_{1};x_{1}^{\mathbf{i}_{1}},z_{1})\varphi(z_{1})\sigma_{1}+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}\Biggr)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\Biggl(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=1\atop\scriptstyle 2\leq\ell\leq m}\overline{{\mathfrak{s}}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(v_{\ell})\Biggr)(\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{1/2})^{\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}\#_{\ell}}\left(\prod_{\ell=1}^{m-1}\frac{1}{2u_{\ell+1}+u_{\ell}}\right).

(6.67)

Here, when
\widehat{\widetilde{\sigma}}_{1}=1
, the prefactor
\Lambda_{\varepsilon}
of
\Lambda_{\varepsilon}P_{t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{\tau}_{1}+u_{1}}(\widetilde{x}_{1}^{1},x_{1}^{2}+{\varepsilon}{\sqrt{2}}x_{1}^{\mathbf{i}_{1}})
emerges from the edge weights of
\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}^{\widetilde{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}}_{{\varepsilon};1}
.
To analyze the right-hand side of (
6.67
), we apply (
6.45
) and (
6.53
) to obtain the following two bounds:

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{4}}\begin{bmatrix}{\mathrm{d}}x^{3}_{1}\\
{\mathrm{d}}x^{2}_{1}\end{bmatrix}_{\otimes}\varphi(x^{\mathbf{i}_{1}}_{1})\begin{bmatrix}P_{t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{\tau}_{1}+u_{1}}\left(\frac{\widetilde{z}_{1}^{\prime}-{\varepsilon}\widetilde{z}_{1}}{{\sqrt{2}}},x_{1}^{2}+{\varepsilon}{\sqrt{2}}x_{1}^{\mathbf{i}_{1}}\right)\\
P_{u_{1}}(x_{0}^{2},x_{1}^{2})\end{bmatrix}_{\times}

\displaystyle\times\Biggl(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}z_{1}{\mathfrak{s}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(0,v_{1};x_{1}^{\mathbf{i}_{1}},z_{1})\varphi(z_{1})\sigma_{1}+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}\Biggr)

\displaystyle\quad=\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\overline{z}\phi(\overline{z})P_{t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{\tau}_{1}+2u_{1}}\left(\frac{\widetilde{z}_{1}^{\prime}-{\varepsilon}\widetilde{z}_{1}}{{\sqrt{2}}}-{\varepsilon}\overline{z},x_{0}^{2}\right)\Biggl(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}z_{1}{\mathfrak{s}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(0,v_{1};\overline{z}/{\sqrt{2}},z_{1})\varphi(z_{1})\sigma_{1}+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}\Biggr)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\overline{z}\frac{\phi(\overline{z})}{t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{\tau}_{1}+2u_{1}}\Biggl(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}z_{1}{\mathfrak{s}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(0,v_{1};\overline{z}/{\sqrt{2}},z_{1})\varphi(z_{1})\sigma_{1}+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}\Biggr)

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{\overline{{\mathfrak{s}}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(v_{1})\sigma_{1}+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}}{t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{\tau}_{1}+2u_{1}},

(6.68)

and

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{4}}\begin{bmatrix}{\mathrm{d}}x^{3}_{1}\\
{\mathrm{d}}x^{2}_{1}\end{bmatrix}_{\otimes}\varphi(x^{\mathbf{i}_{1}}_{1})\begin{bmatrix}\Lambda_{\varepsilon}P_{t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{\tau}_{1}+u_{1}}(\widetilde{x}_{1}^{1},x_{1}^{2}+{\varepsilon}{\sqrt{2}}x_{1}^{\mathbf{i}_{1}})\\
P_{u_{1}}(x_{0}^{2},x_{1}^{2})\end{bmatrix}_{\times}\Biggl(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}z_{1}{\mathfrak{s}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(0,v_{1};x_{1}^{\mathbf{i}_{1}},z_{1})\varphi(z_{1})\sigma_{1}+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}\Biggr)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\Lambda_{\varepsilon}\frac{\overline{{\mathfrak{s}}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(v_{1})\sigma_{1}+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}}{t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{\tau}_{1}+2u_{1}}.

(6.69)

Additionally, the remainder of the right-hand side of (
6.67
) can be simplified by
using (
6.53
) and (
6.64
). Hence, by (
6.67
)–(
6.69
), we obtain the following bound:

\displaystyle\int{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}^{\widetilde{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}}_{{\varepsilon};0}\int{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}^{\widetilde{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}}_{{\varepsilon};1}\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};0}\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};1}\cdots\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};m}\mathds{1}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\overline{z}}P_{2\widetilde{s}_{1}}(\widetilde{x}_{0}^{2}-{\varepsilon}\widetilde{\overline{z}},\widetilde{x}_{0}^{1})\phi(\widetilde{\overline{z}})\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{z}_{1}{\mathfrak{s}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(\widetilde{s}_{1},\widetilde{\tau}_{1};\widetilde{\overline{z}}/{\sqrt{2}},\widetilde{z}_{1})\varphi(\widetilde{z}_{1})

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\Biggl(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=1\atop\scriptstyle 1\leq\ell\leq m}\overline{{\mathfrak{s}}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(v_{\ell})\Biggr)\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{\lVert\widehat{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}\rVert}\left(\frac{1}{t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{\tau}_{1}+2u_{1}}\right)\left(\prod_{\ell=1}^{m-1}\frac{1}{2u_{\ell+1}+u_{\ell}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\widehat{\widetilde{\sigma}}_{1}\Lambda_{\varepsilon}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\overline{z}}P_{2\widetilde{s}_{1}}(\widetilde{x}_{0}^{2}-{\varepsilon}\widetilde{\overline{z}},\widetilde{x}_{0}^{1})\phi(\widetilde{\overline{z}})

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\Biggl(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=1\atop\scriptstyle 1\leq\ell\leq m}\overline{{\mathfrak{s}}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(v_{\ell})\Biggr)\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{\lVert\widehat{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}\rVert}\left(\frac{1}{t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{\tau}_{1}+2u_{1}}\right)\left(\prod_{\ell=1}^{m-1}\frac{1}{2u_{\ell+1}+u_{\ell}}\right),

which is enough to get (
6.51
) in the case of
\mathbf{i}_{1}=(3,2)
and
m\geq 1
.

To derive (
6.52
) for
\mathbf{i}=(3,2)
and
m\geq 1
, observe that the bound in (
6.67
) can be adjusted to yield the following result:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}^{(\widetilde{B}^{1},\widetilde{B}^{2})}_{(\widetilde{x}_{0}^{1},\widetilde{x}_{0}^{2})}\Bigg[\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};0}\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};1}\cdots\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};m}\mathds{1}\mid_{(x_{0}^{3},x_{0}^{4})=(\widetilde{B}^{1}_{t^{\prime}-t},\widetilde{B}^{2}_{t^{\prime}-t})}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\quad\leq\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{4}}\begin{bmatrix}{\mathrm{d}}x^{3}_{1}\\
{\mathrm{d}}x^{2}_{1}\end{bmatrix}_{\otimes}\varphi(x^{\mathbf{i}_{1}}_{1})\begin{bmatrix}P_{t^{\prime}-t+u_{1}}(\widetilde{x}_{0}^{1},x_{1}^{2}+{\varepsilon}{\sqrt{2}}x_{1}^{\mathbf{i}_{1}})\\
P_{u_{1}}(x_{0}^{2},x_{1}^{2})\end{bmatrix}_{\times}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\Biggl(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}z_{1}{\mathfrak{s}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(0,v_{1};x_{1}^{\mathbf{i}_{1}},z_{1})\varphi(z_{1})\sigma_{1}+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}\Biggr)\Biggl(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=1\atop\scriptstyle 2\leq\ell\leq m}\overline{{\mathfrak{s}}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(v_{\ell})\Biggr)\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{\lVert\widehat{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}\rVert}\left(\prod_{\ell=1}^{m-1}\frac{1}{2u_{\ell+1}+u_{\ell}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\overline{z}P_{t^{\prime}-t+2u_{1}}\left(\widetilde{x}_{0}^{1}-{\varepsilon}\overline{z},x_{0}^{2}\right)\phi(\overline{z})

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\Biggl(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}z_{1}{\mathfrak{s}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(0,v_{1};\overline{z}/{\sqrt{2}},z_{1})\varphi(z_{1})\sigma_{1}+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}\Biggr)\Biggl(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=1\atop\scriptstyle 2\leq\ell\leq m}\overline{{\mathfrak{s}}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(v_{\ell})\Biggr)\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{\lVert\widehat{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}\rVert}\left(\prod_{\ell=1}^{m-1}\frac{1}{2u_{\ell+1}+u_{\ell}}\right).

We have proved all of the bounds stated in Proposition
6.14
. The proof is complete.

\blacksquare

Lemma
6.15
studies some elementary
k
-fold iterated integrals and obtains bounds, given by (
6.71
), which are enough for the forthcoming applications of Proposition
6.14
. More specifically, we will use these bounds simultaneously for all
k\in\mathbb{N}
. Note that (
6.71
) with
T=1
and
p=1/2
slightly improves the second inequality in
[
9
, Lemma 5.4]
by using smaller constants and a more straightforward argument. On the other hand, the method of that proof in
[
9
]
may be of independent interest for its random-walk counting argument.

Lemma 6.15.

For all
p\in(0,1)
and
a\in(0,\infty)
,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}s}{s^{p}(s+a)}\leq\frac{C_{\ref{int:sabddp}}(p)}{a^{p}},

(6.70)

and for all integers
k\geq 1
,
s_{0},T\in(0,\infty)
and
p\in(0,1)
,

\displaystyle\int_{(0,T)^{k}}{\mathrm{d}}(s_{1},\cdots,s_{k})\prod_{j=1}^{k}\frac{1}{2s_{j}+s_{j-1}}\leq C_{\ref{int:sabddp}}(p)^{k}\left(\frac{T}{s_{0}}\right)^{p}.

(6.71)

In particular, we can take
C_{\ref{int:sabddp}}(\tfrac{1}{2})
to be
\pi
.

Remark 6.16.

The fact that the bounds in (
6.71
) allow arbitrary
p\in(0,1)
may suggest an improvement to the logarithmic order. Nevertheless, although the integral in (
6.71
) with
k=1
allows a sharp bound of the order
\log s_{0}^{-1}
as
s_{0}\searrow 0
, the integral with
k=2
is of the order
\log^{2}s_{0}^{-1}
as
s_{0}\searrow 0
. See also the first inequality in
[
9
, Lemma 5.4]
.
\blacklozenge

Proof of Lemma
6.15
.

To prove (
6.70
) for general
p\in(0,1)
, first, we use the change of variables
as^{\prime}=s
to get

\int_{0}^{1}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}s}{s^{p}(s+a)}=\frac{1}{a^{p}}\int_{0}^{1/a}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}s^{\prime}}{(s^{\prime})^{p}(s^{\prime}+1)}.

To bound the right-hand side, note that

\displaystyle\forall\;a\in[1,\infty),\quad\frac{1}{a^{p}}\int_{0}^{1/a}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}s^{\prime}}{(s^{\prime})^{p}(s^{\prime}+1)}

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{1}{a^{p}}\int_{0}^{1/a}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}s^{\prime}}{(s^{\prime})^{p}}\asymp\frac{C(p)}{a}\leq\frac{C(p)}{a^{p}};

\displaystyle\forall\;a\in(0,1),\quad\frac{1}{a^{p}}\int_{1}^{1/a}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}s^{\prime}}{(s^{\prime})^{p}(s^{\prime}+1)}

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{1}{a^{p}}\int_{1}^{1/a}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}s^{\prime}}{(s^{\prime})^{p+1}}\asymp C(p).

For the case of
a\in(0,1)
, we still have
\int_{0}^{1}{\mathrm{d}}s^{\prime}/[(s^{\prime})^{p}(s^{\prime}+1)]
to take into account. Hence, (
6.70
) holds and is sharp as
a\searrow 0
. Also, to see that we can take
C_{\ref{int:sabddp}}(\tfrac{1}{2})
to be
\pi
, it is enough to note the following identity and
\lvert\tan^{-1}(\cdot)\rvert\leq\pi/2
:

\displaystyle\int\frac{{\mathrm{d}}s}{\sqrt{s}(s+a)}=\frac{2}{\sqrt{a}}\tan^{-1}\left(\frac{\sqrt{s}}{\sqrt{a}}\right)+C,\quad s,a\in(0,\infty).

(6.72)

We prove (
6.71
) now. By the change of variables
T\,\widetilde{s}_{j}=s_{j}
for all
j
, it suffices to prove (
6.71
) for
T=1
. In this case, the required inequality with
k=1
holds because

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}s_{1}}{2s_{1}+s_{0}}\leq\int_{0}^{1}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}s_{1}}{s_{1}^{p}(s_{1}+s_{0})}\leq\frac{C_{\ref{int:sabddp}}(p)}{s_{0}^{p}},\quad\forall\;s_{0}\in(0,\infty),

(6.73)

where the first inequality uses the bound
x+y\geq x^{p}y
, which is equivalent to
x+(1-x^{p})y\geq 0
, for all
x\in[0,1]
and
y\geq 0
by taking
x=s_{1}
and
y=s_{1}+s_{0}
, and the last equality uses (
6.70
). To obtain (
6.71
) for
T=1
and all
k\geq 2
and
s_{0}\in(0,\infty)
, note that (
6.73
) and (
6.70
) imply the first and second inequalities below, respectively, where
k^{\prime}\geq 2
:

\displaystyle\int_{(0,1)^{k}}{\mathrm{d}}(s_{1},\cdots,s_{k})\prod_{j=1}^{k}\frac{1}{2s_{j}+s_{j-1}}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{(0,1)^{k-1}}{\mathrm{d}}(s_{1},\cdots,s_{k-1})\frac{C_{\ref{int:sabddp}}(p)}{s_{k-1}^{p}}\prod_{j=1}^{k-1}\frac{1}{2s_{j}+s_{j-1}},

\displaystyle\int_{(0,1)^{k^{\prime}}}{\mathrm{d}}(s_{1},\cdots,s_{k^{\prime}})\frac{1}{s_{k^{\prime}}^{p}}\prod_{j=1}^{k^{\prime}}\frac{1}{2s_{j}+s_{j-1}}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{(0,1)^{k^{\prime}-1}}{\mathrm{d}}(s_{1},\cdots,s_{k^{\prime}-1})\frac{C_{\ref{int:sabddp}}(p)}{s_{k^{\prime}-1}^{p}}\prod_{j=1}^{k^{\prime}-1}\frac{1}{2s_{j}+s_{j-1}}.

By iterating the second inequality, we see that the first inequality gives

\int_{(0,1)^{k}}{\mathrm{d}}(s_{1},\cdots,s_{k})\prod_{j=1}^{k}\frac{1}{2s_{j}+s_{j-1}}\leq\int_{0}^{1}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}s_{1}C_{\ref{int:sabddp}}(p)^{k-1}}{s_{1}^{p}(2s_{1}+s_{0})}\leq\frac{C_{\ref{int:sabddp}}(p)^{k}}{s_{0}^{p}},

where the last inequality uses (
6.70
). We have proved (
6.71
) for
T=1
and all
k\geq 1
and
s_{0}\in(0,\infty)
, and hence, (
6.71
) for general
T
as well.

\blacksquare

We now turn to establishing the main bounds needed for the proof of Theorem
3.4
, with Lemma
6.11
again connecting these bounds to the theorem. Unlike the earlier moment bounds (e.g.,
[
18
]
), (
6.74
) and (
6.76
) stated below involve integrated forms with
\int_{T_{1}}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t\int_{t}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t^{\prime}
. For further details on their application in (
6.74
), see Remark
6.18
following (
6.82
).

Proposition 6.17.

Let
{\varepsilon}\in(0,{{\varepsilon}_{\ref{def:ovl}}(\lambda)}]
,
0<t<t^{\prime}<\infty
,
x_{0}^{1},x_{0}^{2},\widetilde{x}_{0}^{1},\widetilde{x}_{0}^{2}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{2}
with
x_{0}^{2}=x_{0}^{1}+{\varepsilon}x_{0}^{0}
and
\widetilde{x}_{0}^{2}=\widetilde{x}_{0}^{1}+{\varepsilon}\widetilde{x}_{0}^{0}
for
x_{0}^{0},\widetilde{x}_{0}^{0}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{2}
,
m\in\mathbb{N}
,
\widetilde{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}\in\{0,1\},{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}\in\{0,1\}^{m}
, and
\mathbf{i}_{1}\neq\cdots\neq\mathbf{i}_{m}
with
\mathbf{i}_{1},\cdots,\mathbf{i}_{m}\in\mathcal{E}_{4}
. Consider the corresponding iterated graphical integrals in (
6.42
). Additionally, let
q\geq 0
,
0<T<\infty
, and
p\in(0,1)
, and recall
\mathfrak{S}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(q)
defined in (
6.46
).

(1
∘
)

If
\mathbf{i}_{1}=(2,1)
, then for all
0\leq T_{1}<T_{2}\leq T
,

\displaystyle\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{2}\int_{T_{1}}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t\int_{t}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t^{\prime}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}-t}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}_{1}\int_{[\widetilde{s}_{1},t^{\prime}-t)}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\tau_{1}}\int_{\Delta_{m}(t)}{\mathrm{d}}\boldsymbol{u}_{m}\otimes{\mathrm{d}}\boldsymbol{v}_{m}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg(\prod_{\ell:\widetilde{\sigma}_{\ell}=0}\delta_{\widetilde{s}_{\ell}}(\widetilde{\tau}_{\ell})\Bigg)\Bigg(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=0\atop\scriptstyle 1\leq\ell\leq m}\delta_{0}(v_{\ell})\Bigg)\int{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}^{\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}_{{\varepsilon};0}\int{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}^{\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}_{{\varepsilon};1}\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};0}\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};1}\cdots\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};m}\mathds{1}

\displaystyle\quad\leq C(\lambda,\lVert\varphi\rVert_{\infty},q,T,p)(T_{2}-T_{1})^{2-p}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\left(\int_{0}^{T}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}_{1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\overline{z}}P_{2\widetilde{s}_{1}}({\varepsilon}\widetilde{x}_{0}^{0}-{\varepsilon}\widetilde{\overline{z}})\phi(\widetilde{\overline{z}})\right)\left(\int_{0}^{T}{\mathrm{d}}u_{1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\overline{z}P_{2u_{1}}({\varepsilon}x_{0}^{0}-{\varepsilon}\overline{z})\phi(\overline{z})\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times C(T,p)^{m}\mathfrak{S}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(q)^{\lVert{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}\rVert-\sigma_{1}}(\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}+\Lambda_{\varepsilon}\widehat{\widetilde{\sigma}}_{1})\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{2+\lVert\widehat{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}\rVert},

(6.74)

and for all
0<t<t^{\prime}\leq T
,

\displaystyle\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}^{(\widetilde{B}^{1},\widetilde{B}^{2})}_{(\widetilde{x}_{0}^{1},\widetilde{x}_{0}^{2})}\Bigg[\int_{\Delta_{m}(t)}{\mathrm{d}}\boldsymbol{u}_{m}\otimes{\mathrm{d}}\boldsymbol{v}_{m}\Bigg(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=0\atop\scriptstyle 1\leq\ell\leq m}\delta_{0}(v_{\ell})\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad\times\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};0}\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};1}\cdots\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};m}\mathds{1}\mid_{(x_{0}^{3},x_{0}^{4})=(\widetilde{B}^{1}_{t^{\prime}-t},\widetilde{B}^{2}_{t^{\prime}-t})}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{C(\lambda,\lVert\varphi\rVert_{\infty},q,T,p)}{(t^{\prime}-t)^{p}}\left(\int_{0}^{T}{\mathrm{d}}u_{1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\overline{z}P_{2u_{1}}({\varepsilon}x_{0}^{0}-{\varepsilon}\overline{z})\phi(\overline{z})\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times C(T,p)^{m}\mathfrak{S}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(q)^{\lVert{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}\rVert-\sigma_{1}}\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{2+\lVert\widehat{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}\rVert}.

(6.75)

(2
∘
)

If
\mathbf{i}_{1}=(4,3)
or
\mathbf{i}_{1}=(3,2)
, then for all
0\leq T_{1}<T_{2}\leq T
,

\displaystyle\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{2}\int_{T_{1}}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t\int_{t}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t^{\prime}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}-t}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}_{1}\int_{[\widetilde{s}_{1},t^{\prime}-t)}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\tau_{1}}\int_{\Delta_{m}(t)}{\mathrm{d}}\boldsymbol{u}_{m}\otimes{\mathrm{d}}\boldsymbol{v}_{m}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg(\prod_{\ell:\widetilde{\sigma}_{\ell}=0}\delta_{\widetilde{s}_{\ell}}(\widetilde{\tau}_{\ell})\Bigg)\Bigg(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=0\atop\scriptstyle 1\leq\ell\leq m}\delta_{0}(v_{\ell})\Bigg)\int{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}^{\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}_{{\varepsilon};0}\int{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}^{\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}_{{\varepsilon};1}\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};0}\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};1}\cdots\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};m}\mathds{1}

\displaystyle\quad\leq C(\lambda,\lVert\varphi\rVert_{\infty},q,T,p)(T_{2}-T_{1})^{2-p}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{T}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}_{1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\overline{z}}P_{2\widetilde{s}_{1}}({\varepsilon}\widetilde{x}_{0}^{0}-{\varepsilon}\widetilde{\overline{z}})\phi(\widetilde{\overline{z}})\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times C(T,p)^{m}\mathfrak{S}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(q)^{\lVert{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}\rVert}(\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}+\Lambda_{\varepsilon}\widehat{\widetilde{\sigma}}_{1})\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{2+\lVert\widehat{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}\rVert},

(6.76)

and for all
0<t<t^{\prime}\leq T
,

\displaystyle\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}^{(\widetilde{B}^{1},\widetilde{B}^{2})}_{(\widetilde{x}_{0}^{1},\widetilde{x}_{0}^{2})}\Bigg[\int_{\Delta_{m}(t)}{\mathrm{d}}\boldsymbol{u}_{m}\otimes{\mathrm{d}}\boldsymbol{v}_{m}\Bigg(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=0\atop\scriptstyle 1\leq\ell\leq m}\delta_{0}(v_{\ell})\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad\times\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};0}\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};1}\cdots\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};m}\mathds{1}\mid_{(x_{0}^{3},x_{0}^{4})=(\widetilde{B}^{1}_{t^{\prime}-t},\widetilde{B}^{2}_{t^{\prime}-t})}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{C(\lambda,\lVert\varphi\rVert_{\infty},q,T,p)}{(t^{\prime}-t)^{p}}C(T,p)^{m}\mathfrak{S}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(q)^{\lVert{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}\rVert}\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{2+\lVert\widehat{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}\rVert}.

(6.77)

Proof of Proposition
6.17
(1
∘
).

By the present choice of
(x_{0}^{1},x_{0}^{2},\widetilde{x}_{0}^{1},\widetilde{x}_{0}^{2})
, we can write

\displaystyle\widetilde{x}_{0}^{2}-{\varepsilon}\widetilde{\overline{z}}-\widetilde{x}_{0}^{1}={\varepsilon}\widetilde{x}^{0}_{0}-{\varepsilon}\widetilde{\overline{z}},\quad x_{0}^{2}-{\varepsilon}\overline{z}-x_{0}^{1}={\varepsilon}x^{0}_{0}-{\varepsilon}\overline{z}.

(6.78)

Hence, by (
6.49
),

\displaystyle\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{2}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}-t}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}_{1}\int_{[\widetilde{s}_{1},t^{\prime}-t)}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\tau_{1}}\int_{\Delta_{m}(t)}{\mathrm{d}}\boldsymbol{u}_{m}\otimes{\mathrm{d}}\boldsymbol{v}_{m}\Bigg(\prod_{\ell:\widetilde{\sigma}_{\ell}=0}\delta_{\widetilde{s}_{\ell}}(\widetilde{\tau}_{\ell})\Bigg)\Bigg(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=0\atop\scriptstyle 1\leq\ell\leq m}\delta_{0}(v_{\ell})\Bigg)

\displaystyle\times\int{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}^{\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}_{{\varepsilon};0}\int{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}^{\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}_{{\varepsilon};1}\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};0}\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};1}\cdots\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};m}\mathds{1}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{2}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}-t}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}_{1}\int_{[\widetilde{s}_{1},t^{\prime}-t)}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\tau_{1}}\int_{\Delta_{m}(t)}{\mathrm{d}}\boldsymbol{u}_{m}\otimes{\mathrm{d}}\boldsymbol{v}_{m}\Bigg(\prod_{\ell:\widetilde{\sigma}_{\ell}=0}\delta_{\widetilde{s}_{\ell}}(\widetilde{\tau}_{\ell})\Bigg)\Bigg(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=0\atop\scriptstyle 1\leq\ell\leq m}\delta_{0}(v_{\ell})\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\overline{z}}P_{2\widetilde{s}_{1}}({\varepsilon}\widetilde{x}^{0}_{0}-{\varepsilon}\widetilde{\overline{z}})\phi(\widetilde{\overline{z}})\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{z}_{1}{\mathfrak{s}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(\widetilde{s}_{1},\widetilde{\tau}_{1};\widetilde{\overline{z}}/{\sqrt{2}},\widetilde{z}_{1})\varphi(\widetilde{z}_{1})\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}+\widehat{\widetilde{\sigma}}_{1}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\overline{z}P_{2u_{1}}({\varepsilon}x^{0}_{0}-{\varepsilon}\overline{z})\phi(\overline{z})\Biggl(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}z_{1}{\mathfrak{s}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(0,v_{1};\overline{z}/{\sqrt{2}},z_{1})\varphi(z_{1})\sigma_{1}+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}\Biggr)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\Biggl(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=1\atop\scriptstyle 2\leq\ell\leq m}\overline{{\mathfrak{s}}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(v_{\ell})\Biggr)\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{\lVert\widehat{\widetilde{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}}\rVert+\lVert\widehat{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}\rVert}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\left(\frac{\mathds{1}_{m\geq 2}}{t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{\tau}_{1}+2u_{2}+u_{1}}+\mathds{1}_{m=1}\right)\left(\prod_{\ell=2}^{m-1}\frac{1}{2u_{\ell+1}+u_{\ell}}\right).

(6.79)

To simplify the right-hand side of (
6.79
), we analyze the integrals with respect to
\boldsymbol{v}_{m}
and
\boldsymbol{u}_{m}
separately as follows. With
\Delta_{m}(t)_{\boldsymbol{u}_{m}}
denoting the section of the set
\Delta_{m}(t)
, defined in (
6.9
),
for fixed
\boldsymbol{u}_{m}
, we obtain from the simple inequality
1\leq{\mathrm{e}}^{qt}{\mathrm{e}}^{-q\sum_{\ell=1}^{m}v_{\ell}}
for any
q\geq 0
that

\displaystyle\int_{\Delta_{m}(t)_{\boldsymbol{u}_{m}}}{\mathrm{d}}\boldsymbol{v}_{m}\Bigg(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=0\atop\scriptstyle 1\leq\ell\leq m}\delta_{0}(v_{\ell})\Bigg)\Biggl(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}z_{1}{\mathfrak{s}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(0,v_{1};\overline{z}/{\sqrt{2}},z_{1})\varphi(z_{1})\sigma_{1}+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}\Biggr)\Biggl(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=1\atop\scriptstyle 2\leq\ell\leq m}\overline{{\mathfrak{s}}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(v_{\ell})\Biggr)

\displaystyle\quad\leq{\mathrm{e}}^{qt}\Biggl(\int_{0}^{t}{\mathrm{d}}v_{1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}z_{1}{\mathfrak{s}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(0,v_{1};\overline{z}/{\sqrt{2}},z_{1})\varphi(z_{1})\sigma_{1}+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}\Biggr)\Biggl(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=1\atop\scriptstyle 2\leq\ell\leq m}\int_{0}^{t}{\mathrm{d}}v_{\ell}{\mathrm{e}}^{-qv_{\ell}}\overline{{\mathfrak{s}}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(v_{\ell})\Biggr)

\displaystyle\quad\leq C(\lambda,\lVert\varphi\rVert_{\infty},q,T)\mathfrak{S}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(q)^{\lVert{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}\rVert-\sigma_{1}},

(6.80)

where the last inequality uses (
6.48
) and the definition (
6.46
) of
\mathfrak{S}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(q)
. Note that the right-hand side of the foregoing inequality is independent of
\boldsymbol{u}_{m}
. Also,

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)^{m}}{\mathrm{d}}\boldsymbol{u}_{m}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\overline{z}P_{2u_{1}}({\varepsilon}x^{0}_{0}-{\varepsilon}\overline{z})\phi(\overline{z})\left(\frac{\mathds{1}_{m\geq 2}}{t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{\tau}_{1}+2u_{2}+u_{1}}+\mathds{1}_{m=1}\right)\left(\prod_{\ell=2}^{m-1}\frac{1}{2u_{\ell+1}+u_{\ell}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\int_{0}^{t}{\mathrm{d}}u_{1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\overline{z}P_{2u_{1}}({\varepsilon}x_{0}^{0}-{\varepsilon}\overline{z})\phi(\overline{z})\Bigg[\int_{(0,t)^{m-1}}{\mathrm{d}}(u_{2},\cdots,u_{m})\left(\frac{\mathds{1}_{m\geq 2}}{t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{\tau}_{1}+2u_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\left(\prod_{\ell=2}^{m-1}\frac{1}{2u_{\ell+1}+u_{\ell}}\right)+\mathds{1}_{m=1}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\quad\leq C(T,p)\int_{0}^{t}{\mathrm{d}}u_{1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\overline{z}P_{2u_{1}}({\varepsilon}x_{0}^{0}-{\varepsilon}\overline{z})\phi(\overline{z})\left(\frac{C(T,p)^{m}}{(t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{\tau}_{1})^{p}}\mathds{1}_{m\geq 2}+\mathds{1}_{m=1}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\leq C(T,p)\int_{0}^{t}{\mathrm{d}}u_{1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\overline{z}P_{2u_{1}}({\varepsilon}x_{0}^{0}-{\varepsilon}\overline{z})\phi(\overline{z})\frac{C(T,p)^{m}}{(t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{\tau}_{1})^{p}},

(6.81)

where the next to the last inequality follows from (
6.71
) with the choice of

k\equiv m-1,\;s_{0}=t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{\tau}_{1},\;s_{1}\equiv u_{2},\;s_{2}\equiv u_{3},\cdots,\;s_{m-1}\equiv u_{m}.

Hence, by considering separately
\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}=1
and
\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}=0
, the above two displays applied to
(
6.79
) give the following bound:

\displaystyle\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{2}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}-t}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}_{1}\int_{[\widetilde{s}_{1},t^{\prime}-t)}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\tau_{1}}\int_{\Delta_{m}(t)}{\mathrm{d}}\boldsymbol{u}_{m}\otimes{\mathrm{d}}\boldsymbol{v}_{m}\Bigg(\prod_{\ell:\widetilde{\sigma}_{\ell}=0}\delta_{\widetilde{s}_{\ell}}(\widetilde{\tau}_{\ell})\Bigg)\Bigg(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=0\atop\scriptstyle 1\leq\ell\leq m}\delta_{0}(v_{\ell})\Bigg)

\displaystyle\times\int{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}^{\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}_{{\varepsilon};0}\int{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}^{\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}_{{\varepsilon};1}\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};0}\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};1}\cdots\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};m}\mathds{1}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}C(\lambda,\lVert\varphi\rVert_{\infty},q,T,p)\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}-t}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}_{1}\int_{\widetilde{s}_{1}}^{t^{\prime}-t}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\tau_{1}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\overline{z}}P_{2\widetilde{s}_{1}}({\varepsilon}\widetilde{x}^{0}_{0}-{\varepsilon}\widetilde{\overline{z}})\phi(\widetilde{\overline{z}})\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{z}_{1}\frac{{\mathfrak{s}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(\widetilde{s}_{1},\widetilde{\tau}_{1};\widetilde{\overline{z}}/{\sqrt{2}},\widetilde{z}_{1})\varphi(\widetilde{z}_{1})}{(t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{\tau}_{1})^{p}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\int_{0}^{t}{\mathrm{d}}u_{1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\overline{z}P_{2u_{1}}({\varepsilon}x_{0}^{0}-{\varepsilon}\overline{z})\phi(\overline{z})C(T,p)^{m}\mathfrak{S}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(q)^{\lVert{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}\rVert-\sigma_{1}}\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{2+\lVert\widehat{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}\rVert}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\widehat{\widetilde{\sigma}}_{1}C(\lambda,\lVert\varphi\rVert_{\infty},q,T,p)\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}-t}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}_{1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\overline{z}}P_{2\widetilde{s}_{1}}({\varepsilon}\widetilde{x}^{0}_{0}-{\varepsilon}\widetilde{\overline{z}})\phi(\widetilde{\overline{z}})\frac{1}{(t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{s}_{1})^{p}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\int_{0}^{t}{\mathrm{d}}u_{1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\overline{z}P_{2u_{1}}({\varepsilon}x_{0}^{0}-{\varepsilon}\overline{z})\phi(\overline{z})C(T,p)^{m}\mathfrak{S}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(q)^{\lVert{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}\rVert-\sigma_{1}}\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{3+\lVert\widehat{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}\rVert}.

(6.82)

Remark 6.18.

To bound the right-hand side of (
6.82
), our main focus is on the first term, especially the integral with respect to
{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}_{1}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\tau}_{1}
. At this stage, it is necessary to introduce
\int_{T_{1}}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t\int_{t}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t^{\prime}
to bypass singularities and deal with a complex convolution specified below.
\blacklozenge

To proceed, we show the following bound: for
0\leq T_{1}<T_{2}\leq T
,

\displaystyle\int_{T_{1}}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t\int_{t}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t^{\prime}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}-t}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}_{1}\int_{\widetilde{s}_{1}}^{t^{\prime}-t}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\tau_{1}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\overline{z}}P_{2\widetilde{s}_{1}}({\varepsilon}\widetilde{x}^{0}_{0}-{\varepsilon}\widetilde{\overline{z}})\phi(\widetilde{\overline{z}})\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{z}_{1}\frac{{\mathfrak{s}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(\widetilde{s}_{1},\widetilde{\tau}_{1};\widetilde{\overline{z}}/{\sqrt{2}},\widetilde{z}_{1})\varphi(\widetilde{z}_{1})}{(t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{\tau}_{1})^{p}}

\displaystyle\quad\leq C(\lambda,\lVert\varphi\rVert_{\infty},T,p)(T_{2}-T_{1})^{2-p}\left(\int_{0}^{T}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}_{1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\overline{z}}P_{2\widetilde{s}_{1}}({\varepsilon}\widetilde{x}_{0}^{0}-{\varepsilon}\widetilde{\overline{z}})\phi(\widetilde{\overline{z}})\right).

(6.83)

First, write

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}-t}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}_{1}\int_{\widetilde{s}_{1}}^{t^{\prime}-t}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\tau_{1}}P_{2\widetilde{s}_{1}}({\varepsilon}\widetilde{x}^{0}_{0}-{\varepsilon}\widetilde{\overline{z}})\phi(\widetilde{\overline{z}})\frac{{\mathfrak{s}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(\widetilde{s}_{1},\widetilde{\tau}_{1};\widetilde{\overline{z}}/{\sqrt{2}},\widetilde{z}_{1})\varphi(\widetilde{z}_{1})}{(t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{\tau}_{1})^{p}}

\displaystyle\quad=\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}-t}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}_{1}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{s}_{1}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\tau_{1}}P_{2\widetilde{s}_{1}}({\varepsilon}\widetilde{x}^{0}_{0}-{\varepsilon}\widetilde{\overline{z}})\phi(\widetilde{\overline{z}})\frac{{\mathfrak{s}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(0,\widetilde{\tau}_{1};\widetilde{\overline{z}}/{\sqrt{2}},\widetilde{z}_{1})\varphi(\widetilde{z}_{1})}{(t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{s}_{1}-\widetilde{\tau}_{1})^{p}},

which follows by changing variables. We apply
\int_{T_{1}}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t\int_{t}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t^{\prime}
to both sides of the foregoing equality. Hence,

\displaystyle\int_{T_{1}}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t\int_{t}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t^{\prime}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}-t}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}_{1}\int_{\widetilde{s}_{1}}^{t^{\prime}-t}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\tau_{1}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\overline{z}}P_{2\widetilde{s}_{1}}({\varepsilon}\widetilde{x}^{0}_{0}-{\varepsilon}\widetilde{\overline{z}})\phi(\widetilde{\overline{z}})\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{z}_{1}\frac{{\mathfrak{s}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(\widetilde{s}_{1},\widetilde{\tau}_{1};\widetilde{\overline{z}}/{\sqrt{2}},\widetilde{z}_{1})\varphi(\widetilde{z}_{1})}{(t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{\tau}_{1})^{p}}

\displaystyle\quad=\int_{T_{1}}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t\int_{t}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t^{\prime}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}-t}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}_{1}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{s}_{1}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\tau_{1}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\overline{z}}P_{2\widetilde{s}_{1}}({\varepsilon}\widetilde{x}^{0}_{0}-{\varepsilon}\widetilde{\overline{z}})\phi(\widetilde{\overline{z}})

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{z}_{1}\frac{{\mathfrak{s}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(0,\widetilde{\tau}_{1};\widetilde{\overline{z}}/{\sqrt{2}},\widetilde{z}_{1})\varphi(\widetilde{z}_{1})}{(t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{s}_{1}-\widetilde{\tau}_{1})^{p}}

\displaystyle\quad=\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\overline{z}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{z}_{1}\int_{T_{1}}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t\int_{t}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t^{\prime}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}-t}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}_{1}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{s}_{1}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\tau_{1}}P_{2\widetilde{s}_{1}}({\varepsilon}\widetilde{x}^{0}_{0}-{\varepsilon}\widetilde{\overline{z}})\phi(\widetilde{\overline{z}})

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\frac{{\mathfrak{s}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(0,\widetilde{\tau}_{1};\widetilde{\overline{z}}/{\sqrt{2}},\widetilde{z}_{1})\varphi(\widetilde{z}_{1})}{(t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{s}_{1}-\widetilde{\tau}_{1})^{p}},

(6.84)

and the right-hand side
takes the form of the left-hand side of the following inequality:

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\overline{z}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{z}_{1}\int_{T_{1}}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t\int_{t}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t^{\prime}(f\star g\star h)(t^{\prime}-t)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\overline{z}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{z}_{1}\int_{T_{1}}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t\left(\int_{0}^{T_{2}-t}{\mathrm{d}}t^{\prime}f(t^{\prime})\right)\left(\int_{0}^{T_{2}-t}{\mathrm{d}}t^{\prime}g(t^{\prime})\right)\left(\int_{0}^{T_{2}-t}{\mathrm{d}}t^{\prime}h(t^{\prime})\right).

(6.85)

Specifically, we take
f(t^{\prime})\equiv P_{2t^{\prime}}({\varepsilon}\widetilde{x}^{0}_{0}-{\varepsilon}\widetilde{\overline{z}})\phi(\widetilde{\overline{z}})
,
g(t^{\prime})\equiv{\mathfrak{s}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(0,t^{\prime};\widetilde{\overline{z}}/{\sqrt{2}},\widetilde{z}_{1})\varphi(\widetilde{z}_{1})
and
h(t^{\prime})\equiv 1/(t^{\prime})^{p}
.
Moreover,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T_{2}-t}{\mathrm{d}}t^{\prime}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{z}_{1}{\mathfrak{s}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(0,t^{\prime};\widetilde{\overline{z}}/{\sqrt{2}},\widetilde{z}_{1})\varphi(\widetilde{z}_{1})\leq C(\lambda,\lVert\varphi\rVert_{\infty},T),

(6.86)

\displaystyle\int_{T_{1}}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t\int_{0}^{T_{2}-t}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}t^{\prime}}{(t^{\prime})^{p}}\leq C(p)\int_{T_{1}}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t(T_{2}-t)^{1-p}\leq C(p)(T_{2}-T_{1})^{2-p},

(6.87)

where (
6.86
) uses (
6.48
). Combining (
6.84
)–(
6.87
) then proves (
6.83
).

We are ready to finish the proof of (
6.74
).
By (
6.83
) and
a similar, but simpler, bound for the second term in (
6.82
), we obtain from (
6.82
) that

\displaystyle\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{2}\int_{T_{1}}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t\int_{t}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t^{\prime}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}-t}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}_{1}\int_{[\widetilde{s}_{1},t^{\prime}-t)}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\tau_{1}}\int_{\Delta_{m}(t)}{\mathrm{d}}\boldsymbol{u}_{m}\otimes{\mathrm{d}}\boldsymbol{v}_{m}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg(\prod_{\ell:\widetilde{\sigma}_{\ell}=0}\delta_{\widetilde{s}_{\ell}}(\widetilde{\tau}_{\ell})\Bigg)\Bigg(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=0\atop\scriptstyle 1\leq\ell\leq m}\delta_{0}(v_{\ell})\Bigg)\int{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}^{\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}_{{\varepsilon};0}\int{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}^{\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}_{{\varepsilon};1}\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};0}\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};1}\cdots\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};m}\mathds{1}

\displaystyle\quad\leq C(\lambda,\lVert\varphi\rVert_{\infty},q,T,p)(T_{2}-T_{1})^{2-p}\left(\int_{0}^{T}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}_{1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\overline{z}}P_{2\widetilde{s}_{1}}({\varepsilon}\widetilde{x}_{0}^{0}-{\varepsilon}\widetilde{\overline{z}})\phi(\widetilde{\overline{z}})\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\left(\int_{0}^{T}{\mathrm{d}}u_{1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\overline{z}P_{2u_{1}}({\varepsilon}x_{0}^{0}-{\varepsilon}\overline{z})\phi(\overline{z})\right)C(T,p)^{m}\mathfrak{S}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(q)^{\lVert{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}\rVert-\sigma_{1}}(\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}+\Lambda_{\varepsilon}\widehat{\widetilde{\sigma}}_{1})\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{2+\lVert\widehat{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}\rVert},

as required in (
6.74
).

The proof of (
6.75
) is similar. For
0<t<t^{\prime}\leq T
and
q\geq 0
, we obtain the first inequality below by using (
6.50
) and (
6.78
):

\displaystyle\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}^{(\widetilde{B}^{1},\widetilde{B}^{2})}_{(\widetilde{x}_{0}^{1},\widetilde{x}_{0}^{2})}\Bigg[\int_{\Delta_{m}(t)}{\mathrm{d}}\boldsymbol{u}_{m}\otimes{\mathrm{d}}\boldsymbol{v}_{m}\Bigg(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=0\atop\scriptstyle 1\leq\ell\leq m}\delta_{0}(v_{\ell})\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad\times\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};0}\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};1}\cdots\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};m}\mathds{1}\Big|_{(x_{0}^{3},x_{0}^{4})=(\widetilde{B}^{1}_{t^{\prime}-t},\widetilde{B}^{2}_{t^{\prime}-t})}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\quad\leq\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{2}\int_{\Delta_{m}(t)}{\mathrm{d}}\boldsymbol{u}_{m}\otimes{\mathrm{d}}\boldsymbol{v}_{m}\Bigg(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=0\atop\scriptstyle 1\leq\ell\leq m}\delta_{0}(v_{\ell})\Bigg)\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\overline{z}P_{2u_{1}}({\varepsilon}x_{0}^{0}-{\varepsilon}\overline{z})\phi(\overline{z})

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\Biggl(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}z_{1}{\mathfrak{s}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(0,v_{1};\overline{z}/{\sqrt{2}},z_{1})\varphi(z_{1})\sigma_{1}+\widehat{\sigma}_{1}\Biggr)\Biggl(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=1\atop\scriptstyle 2\leq\ell\leq m}\overline{{\mathfrak{s}}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(v_{\ell})\Biggr)\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{\lVert\widehat{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}\rVert}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\left(\frac{\mathds{1}_{m\geq 2}}{t^{\prime}-t+2u_{2}+u_{1}}+\mathds{1}_{m=1}\right)\left(\prod_{\ell=2}^{m-1}\frac{1}{2u_{\ell+1}+u_{\ell}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{C(\lambda,\lVert\varphi\rVert_{\infty},q,T,p)}{(t^{\prime}-t)^{p}}\left(\int_{0}^{T}{\mathrm{d}}u_{1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\overline{z}P_{2u_{1}}({\varepsilon}x_{0}^{0}-{\varepsilon}\overline{z})\phi(\overline{z})\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times C(T,p)^{m}\mathfrak{S}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(q)^{\lVert{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}\rVert-\sigma_{1}}\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{2+\lVert\widehat{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}\rVert},

where the last inequality uses (
6.80
) and adjusts the derivation of (
6.81
) by
using (
6.71
) with the choice of

k\equiv m-1,\;s_{0}=t^{\prime}-t,\;s_{1}\equiv u_{2},\;s_{2}\equiv u_{3},\cdots,\;s_{m-1}\equiv u_{m}.

The last inequality proves (
6.75
).

\blacksquare

Proof of Proposition
6.17
(2
∘
).

We first prove (
6.76
). By (
6.51
) and (
6.78
), for
0\leq T_{1}<T_{2}\leq T
and
q\geq 0
,

\displaystyle\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{2}\int_{T_{1}}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t\int_{t}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t^{\prime}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}-t}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}_{1}\int_{[\widetilde{s}_{1},t^{\prime}-t)}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\tau_{1}}\int_{\Delta_{m}(t)}{\mathrm{d}}\boldsymbol{u}_{m}\otimes{\mathrm{d}}\boldsymbol{v}_{m}\Bigg(\prod_{\ell:\widetilde{\sigma}_{\ell}=0}\delta_{\widetilde{s}_{\ell}}(\widetilde{\tau}_{\ell})\Bigg)\Bigg(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=0\atop\scriptstyle 1\leq\ell\leq m}\delta_{0}(v_{\ell})\Bigg)

\displaystyle\times\int{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}^{\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}_{{\varepsilon};0}\int{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}^{\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}_{{\varepsilon};1}\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};0}\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};1}\cdots\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};m}\mathds{1}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{2}\int_{T_{1}}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t\int_{t}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t^{\prime}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}-t}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}_{1}\int_{[\widetilde{s}_{1},t^{\prime}-t)}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\tau_{1}}\int_{\Delta_{m}(t)}{\mathrm{d}}\boldsymbol{u}_{m}\otimes{\mathrm{d}}\boldsymbol{v}_{m}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\Bigg(\prod_{\ell:\widetilde{\sigma}_{\ell}=0}\delta_{\widetilde{s}_{\ell}}(\widetilde{\tau}_{\ell})\Bigg)\Bigg(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=0\atop\scriptstyle 1\leq\ell\leq m}\delta_{0}(v_{\ell})\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\overline{z}}P_{2\widetilde{s}_{1}}({\varepsilon}\widetilde{x}_{0}^{0}-{\varepsilon}\widetilde{\overline{z}})\phi(\widetilde{\overline{z}})\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{z}_{1}{\mathfrak{s}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(\widetilde{s}_{1},\widetilde{\tau}_{1};\widetilde{\overline{z}}/{\sqrt{2}},\widetilde{z}_{1})\varphi(\widetilde{z}_{1})\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}+\widehat{\widetilde{\sigma}}_{1}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\Biggl(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=1\atop\scriptstyle 1\leq\ell\leq m}\overline{{\mathfrak{s}}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(v_{\ell})\Biggr)\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{\lVert\widehat{\widetilde{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}}\rVert+\lVert\widehat{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}\rVert}\left(\frac{1}{t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{\tau}_{1}+u_{1}}\right)\left(\prod_{\ell=1}^{m-1}\frac{1}{2u_{\ell+1}+u_{\ell}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\leq C(\lambda,\lVert\varphi\rVert_{\infty},q,T,p)\int_{T_{1}}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t\int_{t}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t^{\prime}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}-t}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}_{1}\int_{[\widetilde{s}_{1},t^{\prime}-t)}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\tau_{1}}\Bigg(\prod_{\ell:\widetilde{\sigma}_{\ell}=0}\delta_{\widetilde{s}_{\ell}}(\widetilde{\tau}_{\ell})\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\overline{z}}P_{2\widetilde{s}_{1}}({\varepsilon}\widetilde{x}_{0}^{0}-{\varepsilon}\widetilde{\overline{z}})\phi(\widetilde{\overline{z}})\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{z}_{1}{\mathfrak{s}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(\widetilde{s}_{1},\widetilde{\tau}_{1};\widetilde{\overline{z}}/{\sqrt{2}},\widetilde{z}_{1})\varphi(\widetilde{z}_{1})\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}+\widehat{\widetilde{\sigma}}_{1}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\left(\frac{1}{(t^{\prime}-t-\widetilde{\tau}_{1})^{p}}\right)C(T,p)^{m}\mathfrak{S}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(q)^{\lVert{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}\rVert}(\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}+\Lambda_{\varepsilon}\widehat{\widetilde{\sigma}}_{1})\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{2+\lVert\widehat{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}\rVert}

\displaystyle\quad\leq C(\lambda,\lVert\varphi\rVert_{\infty},q,T,p)(T_{2}-T_{1})^{2-p}\left(\int_{0}^{T}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}_{1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\overline{z}}P_{2\widetilde{s}_{1}}({\varepsilon}\widetilde{x}_{0}^{0}-{\varepsilon}\widetilde{\overline{z}})\phi(\widetilde{\overline{z}})\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times C(T,p)^{m}\mathfrak{S}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(q)^{\lVert{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}\rVert}(\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}+\Lambda_{\varepsilon}\widehat{\widetilde{\sigma}}_{1})\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{2+\lVert\widehat{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}\rVert}.

where the second and third inequalities follow by modifying the argument after (
6.79
) until (
6.87
). The last inequality proves (
6.76
).

Finally, we show (
6.77
). By (
6.52
), for all
0<t<t^{\prime}\leq T
,

\displaystyle\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}^{(\widetilde{B}^{1},\widetilde{B}^{2})}_{(\widetilde{x}_{0}^{1},\widetilde{x}_{0}^{2})}\Bigg[\int_{\Delta_{m}(t)}{\mathrm{d}}\boldsymbol{u}_{m}\otimes{\mathrm{d}}\boldsymbol{v}_{m}\Bigg(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=0\atop\scriptstyle 1\leq\ell\leq m}\delta_{0}(v_{\ell})\Bigg)\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};0}\cdots\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};m}\mathds{1}\Big|_{(x_{0}^{3},x_{0}^{4})=(\widetilde{B}^{1}_{t^{\prime}-t},\widetilde{B}^{2}_{t^{\prime}-t})}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\quad\leq\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{2}\int_{\Delta_{m}(t)}{\mathrm{d}}\boldsymbol{u}_{m}\otimes{\mathrm{d}}\boldsymbol{v}_{m}\Bigg(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=0\atop\scriptstyle 1\leq\ell\leq m}\delta_{0}(v_{\ell})\Bigg)\Biggl(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=1\atop\scriptstyle 1\leq\ell\leq m}\overline{{\mathfrak{s}}}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(v_{\ell})\Biggr)\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{\lVert\widehat{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}\rVert}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\left(\frac{1}{t^{\prime}-t+u_{1}}\right)\left(\prod_{\ell=1}^{m-1}\frac{1}{2u_{\ell+1}+u_{\ell}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{C(\lambda,\lVert\varphi\rVert_{\infty},q,T,p)}{(t^{\prime}-t)^{p}}C(T,p)^{m}\mathfrak{S}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(q)^{\lVert{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}\rVert}\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{2+\lVert\widehat{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}\rVert},

where is obtained by adjusting the proof of (
6.75
).
We have proved (
6.77
).

\blacksquare

We now prove Theorem
3.4
. Since
X_{0}(\cdot)

\displaystyle\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}^{(\widetilde{B}^{1},\widetilde{B}^{2})}_{(\widetilde{x}_{0}^{1},\widetilde{x}_{0}^{1}+{\varepsilon}\widetilde{x}_{0}^{0})}\Bigg[\exp\Bigg\{\Lambda_{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}-t}{\mathrm{d}}r\varphi_{\varepsilon}(\widetilde{B}^{\mathbf{i}_{0}}_{r})\Bigg\}\widetilde{\mathds{1}}_{t}(\widetilde{B}_{t^{\prime}-t})\Bigg]

\displaystyle\quad\leq\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{2}+\widehat{\widetilde{\sigma}}_{1}\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{3}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}-t}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}_{1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{4}}\begin{bmatrix}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{x}_{1}^{2}\\

P_{0,\widetilde{s}_{1}}(\widetilde{x}_{0}^{1},\widetilde{x}_{1}^{1})\end{bmatrix}_{\times}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{2}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}-t}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}_{1}\int_{\widetilde{s}_{1}}^{t^{\prime}-t}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\tau}_{1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{4}}\begin{bmatrix}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{x}_{1}^{2}\\

P_{0,\widetilde{s}_{1}}(\widetilde{x}_{0}^{1},\widetilde{x}_{1}^{1})\end{bmatrix}_{\times}

\displaystyle\int_{T_{1}}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t\int_{t}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t^{\prime}\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}^{(\widetilde{B}^{1},\widetilde{B}^{2})}_{(\widetilde{x}_{0}^{1},\widetilde{x}_{0}^{1}+{\varepsilon}\widetilde{x}_{0}^{0})}\Bigg[\exp\Bigg\{\Lambda_{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}-t}{\mathrm{d}}r\varphi_{\varepsilon}(\widetilde{B}^{\mathbf{i}_{0}}_{r})\Bigg\}\widetilde{\mathds{1}}_{t}(B_{t^{\prime}-t})\Bigg]

(6.89)

Next, we analyze the

\displaystyle C(\lambda,\lVert\varphi\rVert_{\infty},q,T,p)(T_{2}-T_{1})^{2-p}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{T}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}_{1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\overline{z}}P_{2\widetilde{s}_{1}}(-{\varepsilon}\widetilde{x}_{0}^{0}-{\varepsilon}\widetilde{\overline{z}})\phi(\widetilde{\overline{z}})\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad\times C(T,p)^{m}\mathfrak{S}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(q)^{\lVert{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}\rVert}(\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}+\Lambda_{\varepsilon}\widehat{\widetilde{\sigma}}_{1})\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{2+\lVert\widehat{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}\rVert}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times C(T,p)^{m}\mathfrak{S}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(q)^{\lVert{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}\rVert}(\widetilde{\sigma}_{1}+\Lambda_{\varepsilon}\widehat{\widetilde{\sigma}}_{1})\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{2+\lVert\widehat{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}\rVert},

We proceed to an explicit bound of the
\int_{T_{1}}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t\int_{t}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t^{\prime}

\displaystyle\sum_{m=1}^{\infty}\sum_{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\scriptstyle\mathbf{i}_{1},\cdots,\mathbf{i}_{m}\in\mathcal{E}_{4}}}{{\mathbf{i}_{1}\neq\cdots\neq\mathbf{i}_{m}}}}\sum_{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}\in\{0,1\}^{m}}\int_{T_{1}}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t\int_{t}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t^{\prime}\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}^{(\widetilde{B}^{1},\widetilde{B}^{2})}_{(\widetilde{x}_{0}^{1},\widetilde{x}_{0}^{1}+{\varepsilon}\widetilde{x}_{0}^{0})}\Bigg[\int_{\Delta_{m}(t)}{\mathrm{d}}\boldsymbol{u}_{m}\otimes{\mathrm{d}}\boldsymbol{v}_{m}\Biggl(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=0\atop\scriptstyle 1\leq\ell\leq m}\delta_{0}(v_{\ell})\Biggr)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\sum_{m=1}^{\infty}\sum_{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\scriptstyle\mathbf{i}_{1},\cdots,\mathbf{i}_{m}\in\mathcal{E}_{4}}}{{\mathbf{i}_{1}\neq\cdots\neq\mathbf{i}_{m}}}}C(\lambda,\lVert\varphi\rVert_{\infty},q,T,p)(T_{2}-T_{1})^{2-p}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\left(\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{2}\int_{0}^{T}{\mathrm{d}}u_{1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\overline{z}P_{2u_{1}}({\varepsilon}x_{0}^{0}-{\varepsilon}\overline{z})\phi(\overline{z})+\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{2}\right)C(T,p)^{m}\left(\mathfrak{S}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(q)+\Lambda_{\varepsilon}\right)^{m}\left(\mathfrak{S}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(q)^{-1}\vee 1\right)

\displaystyle{4\choose 2}\cdot C(T,p)\cdot(\mathfrak{S}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(q_{\ref{q:modulus}})+\Lambda_{\varepsilon})<\frac{1}{2}\quad\mbox{and}\quad\mathfrak{S}^{\lambda,\phi}_{\varepsilon}(q_{\ref{q:modulus}})\geq\frac{1}{10}\cdot\frac{4\pi}{\log(q_{\ref{q:modulus}}/\beta)}.

\displaystyle\quad\times\Biggl(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=0\atop\scriptstyle 1\leq\ell\leq m}\delta_{0}(v_{\ell})\Biggr)\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};0}\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};1}\cdots\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};m}\mathds{1}\Big|_{(x_{0}^{3},x_{0}^{4})=(\widetilde{B}^{1}_{t^{\prime}-t},\widetilde{B}^{2}_{t^{\prime}-t})}\Bigg]

Moreover, a similar argument shows the following bound for the
\int_{T_{1}}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t\int_{t}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t^{\prime}

\displaystyle\sum_{m=1}^{\infty}\sum_{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\scriptstyle\mathbf{i}_{1},\cdots,\mathbf{i}_{m}\in\mathcal{E}_{4}}}{{\mathbf{i}_{1}\neq\cdots\neq\mathbf{i}_{m}}}}\sum_{\scriptstyle\widetilde{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}\in\{0,1\}\atop\scriptstyle{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}\in\{0,1\}^{m}}\Lambda_{\varepsilon}^{2}\int_{T_{1}}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t\int_{t}^{T_{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t^{\prime}\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}-t}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}_{1}\int_{[\widetilde{s}_{1},t^{\prime}-t)}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\tau}_{1}\int_{\Delta_{m}(t)}{\mathrm{d}}\boldsymbol{u}_{m}\otimes{\mathrm{d}}\boldsymbol{v}_{m}

\displaystyle\times\Biggl(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\widetilde{\sigma}_{\ell}=0\atop\scriptstyle\ell=1}\delta_{\widetilde{s}_{\ell}}(\widetilde{\tau}_{\ell})\Biggr)\Biggl(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=0\atop\scriptstyle 1\leq\ell\leq m}\delta_{0}(v_{\ell})\Biggr)\int{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}^{\widetilde{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}}_{{\varepsilon};0}\int{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}^{\widetilde{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}}}_{{\varepsilon};1}\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};0}\cdots\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};m}\mathds{1}

\displaystyle\quad\leq C(\lambda,\phi,T,p)\biggl[\biggl(\Lambda_{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{T}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{s}_{1}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\widetilde{\overline{z}}P_{2\widetilde{s}_{1}}({\varepsilon}\widetilde{x}_{0}^{0}-{\varepsilon}\widetilde{\overline{z}})\phi(\widetilde{\overline{z}})\biggr)

Proof of Theorem
3.4
(2
∘
).

By (
6.1
), (
6.4
) and (
6.8
), it holds that, for all
t,a,a^{\prime}\in(0,T]
,

\displaystyle\Lambda_{\varepsilon}{\mathbb{E}}_{(\widetilde{x}_{0}^{1},\widetilde{x}_{0}^{1}+{\varepsilon}\widetilde{x}_{0}^{0})}^{(\widetilde{B}^{1},\widetilde{B}^{2})}{\mathbb{E}}\left[X_{\varepsilon}(x_{0}^{1},t)X_{\varepsilon}(x_{0}^{1}+{\varepsilon}x_{0}^{0},t)X_{\varepsilon}(B^{1}_{a},t)X_{\varepsilon}(B^{2}_{a^{\prime}},t)\right]

\displaystyle\quad\leq\Lambda_{\varepsilon}\lVert X_{0}\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\lVert X_{0}\rVert_{\infty}\sum_{m=1}^{\infty}\sum_{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\scriptstyle\mathbf{i}_{1},\cdots,\mathbf{i}_{m}\in\mathcal{E}_{4}}}{{\mathbf{i}_{1}\neq\cdots\neq\mathbf{i}_{m}}}}\sum_{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}\in\{0,1\}^{m}}\Lambda_{\varepsilon}{\mathbb{E}}^{(\widetilde{B}^{1},\widetilde{B}^{2})}_{(\widetilde{x}_{0}^{1},\widetilde{x}_{0}^{1}+{\varepsilon}\widetilde{x}_{0}^{0})}\Bigg[\int_{\Delta_{m}(t)}{\mathrm{d}}\boldsymbol{u}_{m}\otimes{\mathrm{d}}\boldsymbol{v}_{m}\Bigg(\prod_{\scriptstyle\ell:\sigma_{\ell}=0\atop\scriptstyle 1\leq\ell\leq m}\delta_{0}(v_{\ell})\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};0}\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};1}\cdots\int{\mathrm{d}}\mathcal{G}^{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{{\varepsilon};m}\mathds{1}\Big|_{(x_{0}^{3},x_{0}^{4})=(\widetilde{B}^{1}_{a},\widetilde{B}^{2}_{a^{\prime}})}\Bigg],

where the graphical integrals being taken as expectations are subject to the initial condition of
(x_{0}^{1},x_{0}^{2},x_{0}^{3},x_{0}^{4})
with
x_{0}^{2}=x_{0}^{1}+{\varepsilon}x_{0}^{0}
.

When
a^{\prime}=a=a_{0}
, we apply (
6.75
), (
6.77
), and the extension of (
6.77
) mentioned in the proof of (1
∘
), dividing each by
\Lambda_{\varepsilon}
, and selecting
t^{\prime}-t=a_{0}
. With the same choice of
q_{\ref{q:modulus}}=q_{\ref{q:modulus}}(\lambda,\varphi)
and
{\varepsilon}(\lambda,\phi,q_{\ref{q:modulus}})\in(0,1)
as in (

), we obtain (
3.31
) for all
{\varepsilon}\in(0,{\varepsilon}(\lambda,\phi,q_{\ref{q:modulus}}))
. By (
6.1
), this establishes (
3.31
) for all
{\varepsilon}\in(0,{{\varepsilon}_{\ref{def:ovl}}(\lambda)}]
when
a^{\prime}=a=a_{0}
. Moreover, the right-hand side of (
3.31
) in this case is independent of
(\widetilde{x}_{0}^{1},\widetilde{x}_{0}^{1}+{\varepsilon}\widetilde{x}_{0}^{0})
. Therefore, for
a<a^{\prime}
, we can apply the Markov property of
\widetilde{B}^{2}
at time

and use the result established for

to obtain (

). This argument extends (
3.31
) to all

.

\blacksquare

Proofs of Theorem
3.4
(3
∘
) and (
3.33
).

For
N=2
, the infinite series in (

) reduces to just one term, corresponding to

; see Lemma

. Therefore, the proof of Theorem

(1
